namespace GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Services.Spectrometer
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 鍏夎氨浠鐞嗕腑蹇冿細璐熻矗纭欢姹犵殑鎵弿銆佸垵濮嬪寲銆佹暟鎹仛鍚堜笌鍏ㄥ眬璺敱銆?    /// </summary>
    public class SpectrometerManager : IDisposable
    {
        #region 1. 鍗曚緥涓庡苟鍙戝熀寤?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鑾峰彇鍏夎氨浠鐞嗗櫒鐨勫叏灞€鍞竴鍗曚緥瀹炰緥銆?        /// </summary>
        public static SpectrometerManager Instance { get; } = new SpectrometerManager();

        /// <summary>
        /// 鐢ㄤ簬澶氱嚎绋嬬幆澧冧笅鍚屾鎿嶄綔 ObservableCollection 鐨勯攣瀵硅薄銆?        /// </summary>
        private readonly object _devicesLock = new object();

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏆傚瓨澶氬彴璁惧鍦ㄥ悓涓€閲囬泦鍛ㄦ湡鍐呯殑鍏夎氨鏁版嵁蹇収锛圞ey 涓哄簭鍒楀彿锛夈€?        /// 鐢ㄤ簬澶氭満鑱旇皟鍦烘櫙涓嬬殑鏁版嵁瀵归綈涓庢嫾鎺ュ鐞嗐€?        /// </summary>
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, SpectralData> _latestDataCache = new();

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏍囪瘑搴曞眰鍔ㄦ€佸簱 SDK 鏄惁宸插畬鎴愬叏灞€鍒濆鍖栥€?        /// </summary>
        private bool _isInitialized = false;

        /// <summary>
        /// 褰撳墠绯荤粺涓墍鏈夊凡鍦ㄧ嚎骞舵帴鍏ョ鐞嗙殑鍗曟満鍏夎氨浠湇鍔″垪琛ㄣ€?        /// </summary>
        public ObservableCollection<ISpectrometerService> Devices { get; } = new();

        /// <summary>
        /// 绉佹湁鏋勯€犲嚱鏁般€傞厤缃法绾跨▼闆嗗悎鍚屾锛屼繚闅滃绾跨▼娣诲姞璁惧鏃朵笉寮曞彂 UI 宕╂簝銆?        /// </summary>
        private SpectrometerManager()
        {
            // 鍚敤 WPF 闆嗗悎鍚屾鍔熻兘锛屽厑璁稿湪鍚庡彴绾跨▼瀹夊叏淇敼 Devices 闆嗗悎
            BindingOperations.EnableCollectionSynchronization(Devices, _devicesLock);
        }

        #endregion

        #region 2. 鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡涓庢€荤嚎鎺у埗

        /// <summary>
        /// 寮傛鎵弿 USB 鎬荤嚎锛屽垵濮嬪寲 SDK 骞跺皢鏂板彂鐜扮殑鐗╃悊璁惧鍚屾鑷崇鐞嗘睜銆?        /// </summary>
        /// <returns>杩斿洖褰撳墠鎴愬姛鎺ュ叆绠＄悊鐨勫厜璋变华鎬绘暟銆傚垵濮嬪寲澶辫触杩斿洖 -1銆?/returns>
        public async Task<int> DiscoverAndInitDevicesAsync()
        {
            return await Task.Run(() =>
            {
                // 鍏ㄥ眬 SDK 鐜婵€娲?                if (!_isInitialized)
                {
                    int initResult = AvantesSdk.AVS_Init(0); // 绔彛鍙傛暟閫氬父涓?0
                    if (initResult < 0) return -1;
                    _isInitialized = true;
                }

                // 鍒锋柊 USB 璁惧鍒楄〃
                AvantesSdk.AVS_UpdateUSBDevices();

                // 鎺㈡祴褰撳墠杩炴帴鐨勮澶囨暟閲忎笌鍐呭瓨绌洪棿
                uint listSize = 0;
                uint requiredSize = 0;
                AvantesSdk.AVS_GetList(listSize, ref requiredSize, null!);

                if (requiredSize == 0)
                {
                    SyncDevices(Array.Empty<AvantesSdk.AvsIdentityType>());
                    return 0;
                }

                // 鏍规嵁 SDK 瑕佹眰鍒嗛厤鍐呭瓨骞舵寮忔姄鍙栬澶囪韩浠戒俊鎭?                var deviceList = new AvantesSdk.AvsIdentityType[requiredSize / (uint)System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(AvantesSdk.AvsIdentityType))];
                AvantesSdk.AVS_GetList(requiredSize, ref requiredSize, deviceList);

                // 鎵ц澧為噺鍚屾锛屽皢搴曞眰缁撴瀯浣撳寘瑁呬负鏈嶅姟瀹炰緥
                SyncDevices(deviceList);

                return Devices.Count;
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎺у埗姹犲唴鎵€鏈夊凡鍒濆鍖栫殑鍏夎氨浠紑鍚寔缁噰闆嗘ā寮忋€?        /// </summary>
        public void StartAll()
        {
            // 鍏堝叏閲忎笅鍙戞寚浠わ紝鍐嶇粺涓€鍚姩锛屾秷闄ら€愪釜Sleep鐨勬椂搴忓樊
            var deviceList = Devices.ToList();
            foreach (var device in deviceList)
            {
                device.StartContinuousMeasurement();
            }
            Thread.Sleep(50);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎺у埗姹犲唴鎵€鏈夊湪绾胯澶囧仠姝㈤噰闆嗐€?        /// </summary>
        public void StopAll()
        {
            foreach (var device in Devices)
            {
                device.StopMeasurement();                
                Thread.Sleep(100);  // 缁欎簣搴曞眰鍙ユ焺閲婃斁 IO 浜嬪姟鐨勬椂闂?            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏂紑鎵€鏈夌‖浠惰繛鎺ワ紝閿€姣佹墍鏈夋湇鍔″疄渚嬪苟褰诲簳鍗歌浇 SDK 鍏ㄥ眬璧勬簮銆?        /// </summary>
        public void DisconnectAll()
        {
            StopAll();

            foreach (var device in Devices)
            {
                // 绉婚櫎涓ぎ鏁版嵁璺敱鐩戝惉
                device.DataReady -= OnDeviceDataReady;
                device.Dispose();
            }

            // 褰诲簳閿€姣?SDK 瀹炰緥锛岄噴鏀?DLL 璧勬簮
            if (_isInitialized)
            {
                AvantesSdk.AVS_Done();
                _isInitialized = false;
            }

            Devices.Clear();
            _latestDataCache.Clear();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 璧勬簮娓呯悊銆?        /// </summary>
        public void Dispose()
        {
            DisconnectAll();
        }

        #endregion

        #region 3. 璁惧鍚屾涓庝簨浠舵寕杞?
        /// <summary>
        /// 灏嗘壂鎻忓埌鐨勭‖浠舵爣璇嗘暟缁勫悓姝ヨ嚦鎵樼鍒楄〃锛屽苟寤虹珛涓ぎ鏁版嵁璺敱銆?        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="newIdentities">搴曞眰鍥炰紶鐨勮澶囪韩浠藉垪琛ㄣ€?/param>
        private void SyncDevices(AvantesSdk.AvsIdentityType[] newIdentities)
        {
            lock (_devicesLock)
            {
                // 鎵惧嚭鏂紑鐨勮澶囧苟绉婚櫎
                var newSerialNumbers = newIdentities.Select(i => i.m_SerialNumber).ToHashSet();
                var disconnectedDevices = Devices.Where(d => d != null && d.Config != null && !newSerialNumbers.Contains(d.Config.SerialNumber)).ToList();
                
                foreach (var d in disconnectedDevices)
                {
                    d.DataReady -= OnDeviceDataReady;
                    d.Dispose();
                    Devices.Remove(d);
                }

                // 娓呯悊浠讳綍寮傚父浜х敓鐨?null 鍗犱綅绗?                var nullDevices = Devices.Where(d => d == null || d.Config == null).ToList();
                foreach (var nd in nullDevices)
                {
                    Devices.Remove(nd);
                }

                foreach (var identity in newIdentities)
                {
                    // 宸茶繛鎺ョ殑搴忓垪鍙蜂笉閲嶅娣诲姞
                    if (Devices.Any(d => d != null && d.Config != null && d.Config.SerialNumber == identity.m_SerialNumber))
                        continue;

                    // 瀹炰緥鍖栬澶囬厤缃笌椹卞姩鏈嶅姟
                    var config = new SpectrometerConfig
                    {
                        SerialNumber = identity.m_SerialNumber,
                        DeviceName = identity.m_UserFriendlyName
                    };

                    var service = new SpectrometerService(config);

                    // 鎸傝浇缁熶竴鐨勬暟鎹氨缁洖璋冿紝鎵€鏈夋暟鎹涓€绔欓兘浼氭祦鍚?Manager
                    service.DataReady += OnDeviceDataReady;

                    Devices.Add(service);
                }
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region 4. 鏁版嵁鎬荤嚎璺敱寮曟搸

        /// <summary>
        /// 涓ぎ鏁版嵁鎺ユ敹绔欙細璐熻矗瀵规墍鏈夎澶囦骇鍑虹殑鍘熷鏁版嵁杩涜鍒嗘祦銆佸畨鍏ㄦ鏌ヤ笌鍚庡鐞嗐€?        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="data">浠绘剰鐗╃悊鍏夎氨浠骇鐢熺殑鍗曟娴嬮噺蹇収銆?/param>
        private void OnDeviceDataReady(SpectralData data)
        {
            // 瀹夊叏棰勫鐞嗭紙楗卞拰搴﹁繃鏇濇娴嬶級
            HandleSaturationWarning(data);

            //璺敱鍒嗗彂锛堝喅瀹氭槸鍗曢€氶亾閫忎紶杩樻槸鎵ц澶氭満鎷兼帴锛?            HandleDataDispatching(data);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 楗卞拰搴﹀畨鍏ㄦ嫤鎴細妫€娴嬪綋鍓嶅抚鏄惁瀛樺湪鍍忕礌杩囨洕锛屽苟鍚戞€荤嚎鍙戦€佺姸鎬佸憡璀︺€?        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="data">寰呮娴嬬殑鍏夎氨鏁版嵁鍖呫€?/param>
        private void HandleSaturationWarning(SpectralData data)
        {
            var sourceDevice = Devices.FirstOrDefault(d => d.Config.SerialNumber == data.SourceDeviceSerial);

            // 鑻ュ紑鍚簡妫€娴嬪紑鍏筹紝鍒欒皟鐢ㄧ畻娉曞簱妫€鏌?Y 杞村嘲鍊?            if (sourceDevice != null && sourceDevice.Config.IsSaturationDetectionEnabled)
            {
                if (SpectrometerLogic.CheckSaturation(data))
                {
                    // 閫氳繃娑堟伅鎬荤嚎閫氱煡 UI 灞傛樉绀鸿繃鏇濆浘鏍囨垨璀﹀憡
                    WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SpectrometerStatusMessage(sourceDevice.Config));
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏁版嵁鍒嗗彂鍒嗘祦鍣細鏍规嵁褰撳墠鍦ㄧ嚎璁惧鏁伴噺鍐冲畾杈撳嚭妯″紡銆?        /// </summary>
        private void HandleDataDispatching(SpectralData data)
        {
            int deviceCount = Devices.Count;

            // 鍗曟満妯″紡锛氱洿鎺ヨ緭鍑哄師濮嬫暟鎹?            if (deviceCount <= 1)
            {
                DispatchSingleDeviceData(data);
            }
            // 鑱旀満妯″紡锛氭墽琛屽紓姝ュ睆闅滃悓姝ヤ笌鏁版嵁鎷兼帴
            else
            {
                DispatchMultiDeviceStitchedData(data, deviceCount);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎵ц鍗曟満閫忎紶璺敱锛氬皢閲囬泦鏁版嵁灏佽涓烘秷鎭寘鍙戦€佽嚦鍏ㄥ眬銆?        /// </summary>
        private void DispatchSingleDeviceData(SpectralData data)
        {
            var sourceConfig = Devices[0].Config;

            // 鏋勫缓鍖呭惈褰撳墠纭欢閰嶇疆涓婁笅鏂囩殑娑堟伅
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SpectralDataMessage(data)
            {
                IntegrationTime = sourceConfig.IntegrationTimeMs,
                AveragingCount = sourceConfig.AveragingCount
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎵ц澶氭満鎷兼帴璺敱锛氭敹闆嗘墍鏈夎澶囨暟鎹悗鎵ц娉㈤暱瀵归綈涓庢嫾鎺ャ€?        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="data">褰撳墠鍒拌揪鐨勫崟甯ф暟鎹€?/param>
        /// <param name="totalOnlineDevices">鍙備笌鎷兼帴鐨勮澶囨€绘暟銆?/param>
        private void DispatchMultiDeviceStitchedData(SpectralData data, int totalOnlineDevices)
        {
            // 灏嗘渶鏂版暟鎹瓨鍏ョ紦瀛?            _latestDataCache[data.SourceDeviceSerial] = data;

            // 鍙湁褰撴墍鏈夊湪绾胯澶囩殑鏁版嵁閮藉埌杈剧紦瀛樻椂锛屾墠瑙﹀彂鎷兼帴鏁板杩愮畻
            if (_latestDataCache.Count >= totalOnlineDevices)
            {
                // 璋冪敤鏁板閫昏緫灞傛墽琛屽鍏夎氨娈垫嫾鎺?                var combinedData = SpectrometerLogic.PerformStitching(_latestDataCache.Values);

                if (combinedData != null)
                {
                    // 鎷兼帴鍚庣殑娑堟伅鍙傛暟缁熶竴鍙傜収涓昏澶囷紙Index 0锛?                    var mainConfig = Devices[0].Config;
                    WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SpectralDataMessage(combinedData)
                    {
                        IntegrationTime = mainConfig.IntegrationTimeMs,
                        AveragingCount = mainConfig.AveragingCount
                    });
                }

                // 娓呯悊缂撳瓨锛屼负涓嬩竴涓悓姝ュ懆鏈熷仛鍑嗗
                _latestDataCache.Clear();
            }
        }

        #endregion
    }
}
===== C:\Users\JackyAct\FileHub\05_Code\Build\GD_ControlCenter_WPF-main\Services\Spectrometer\SpectrometerService.cs =====
using C_Sharp_Application; 
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.Spectrometer;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

/*
 * 文件名: SpectrometerService.cs
 * 描述: Avantes 光谱仪 SDK 的面向对象封装实现，处理非托管内存回调与多线程硬件访问。
 * 定义了操控单个光谱仪的方法
 * 维护指南: 必须保持 _callback 委托为类成员以规避 GC 回收引发的内存违例。
 */

namespace GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Services.Spectrometer
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 光谱仪单机服务实现类：负责单台设备的生命周期、指令同步及数据流解析。
    /// </summary>
    public partial class SpectrometerService : ObservableObject, ISpectrometerService, IDisposable
    {
        #region 1. 锁与底层资源管理相关字段

        /// <summary>
        /// 全局静态锁对象：用于跨实例保护底层非线程安全的 C++ SDK 指令调用（如启停指令）。
        /// </summary>
        private static readonly object _globalSdkLock = new object();

        /// <summary>
        /// 实例级信号量：保护单台设备的状态机，防止参数更新与测量指令发生冲突。
        /// </summary>
        private readonly SemaphoreSlim _hardwareLock = new SemaphoreSlim(1, 1);

        /// <summary>
        /// 非托管回调委托：必须作为全局成员持有，防止被 .NET 垃圾回收器回收导致 C++ 访问空指针。
        /// </summary>
        private readonly MeasurementCompletedCallback _callback;

        /// <summary>
        /// 异步任务源：用于将异步回调结果（Event）转化为可等待的任务（Task）。
        /// </summary>
        private TaskCompletionSource<SpectralData>? _measureTcs;

        /// <summary>
        /// 持续测量模式的生命周期控制，用于安全中断内部异步逻辑。
        /// </summary>
        private CancellationTokenSource? _continuousCts;

        /// <summary>
        /// 内部标志位：指示当前是否正在执行单次触发测量。
        /// </summary>
        private bool _isSingleMeasuring = false;

        /// <summary>
        /// 内部标志位：指示对象是否已被销毁。
        /// </summary>
        private bool _isDisposed = false;

        #endregion

        #region 2. 接口属性与事件实现

        /// <summary>
        /// 数据准备就绪事件：在非托管线程触发，订阅者需自行处理线程上下文切换。
        /// </summary>
        public event Action<SpectralData>? DataReady;

        /// <summary>
        /// 硬件配置与状态模型。
        /// </summary>
        public SpectrometerConfig Config { get; }

        /// <summary>
        /// 实时指示设备是否处于持续采集状态。
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsMeasuring { get; private set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// 构造光谱仪服务实例。
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="config">光谱仪初始配置实体。</param>
        public SpectrometerService(SpectrometerConfig config)
        {
            Config = config ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(config));

            // 将私有方法绑定到委托成员，固定其内存地址
            _callback = OnMeasurementCompleted;
        }

        #endregion

        #region 3. 生命周期与配置管理逻辑

        /// <summary>
        /// 异步初始化硬件：包含句柄激活、16-bit ADC 协商及像素数探测。
        /// </summary>
        /// <returns>成功连接返回 true。</returns>
        public async Task<bool> InitializeAsync()
        {
            return await Task.Run(() =>
            {
                // 构建 SDK 识别结构体
                var identity = new AvantesSdk.AvsIdentityType
                {
                    m_SerialNumber = Config.SerialNumber,
                    m_UserFriendlyName = Config.DeviceName
                };

                // 调用激活函数，获取硬件操作句柄
                int handle = AvantesSdk.AVS_Activate(ref identity);
                if (handle < AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS) return false;

                // ADC 分辨率协商：优先尝试 16位 (0-65535)，失败则降级到 14位 (0-16383)
                int resHighRes = AvantesSdk.AVS_UseHighResAdc(handle, true);
                if (resHighRes != AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS)
                {
                    // 降级尝试
                    int resLowRes = AvantesSdk.AVS_UseHighResAdc(handle, false);
                    if (resLowRes != AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS)
                    {
                        // 彻底失败时必须反激活句柄，否则串口/USB口将被死锁
                        AvantesSdk.AVS_Deactivate(handle);
                        return false;
                    }
                }

                // 回写配置参数
                Config.DeviceHandle = handle;
                Config.IsConnected = true;

                // 获取物理探测器像素总数（通常为 2048 或 4096）
                ushort pixels = 0;
                AvantesSdk.AVS_GetNumPixels(handle, ref pixels);
                // 设置停止像素索引（像素数 - 1）
                Config.StopPixel = (ushort)(pixels - 1);

                return true;
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 执行参数下发：将托管内存中的积分时间、平均次数、过曝检测等参数写入 SDK 寄存器。
        /// </summary>
        /// <returns>SDK 状态码。</returns>
        public int ApplyConfiguration()
        {
            // 构建底层测量配置结构体
            var measConfig = new AvantesSdk.MeasConfigType
            {
                m_StartPixel = Config.StartPixel,
                m_StopPixel = Config.StopPixel,
                m_IntegrationTime = Config.IntegrationTimeMs,
                m_NrAverages = Config.AveragingCount,
                m_SaturationDetection = (byte)(Config.IsSaturationDetectionEnabled ? 1 : 0),                
                m_Trigger = new AvantesSdk.TriggerType { m_Mode = 0, m_Source = 0, m_SourceType = 0 }   // 使用内部软件触发模式 (Mode=0)
            };

            return AvantesSdk.AVS_PrepareMeasure(Config.DeviceHandle, ref measConfig);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 异步安全更新参数
        /// </summary>
        public async Task<int> UpdateConfigurationAsync(float newIntegrationTime, uint newAverages)
        {
            // 单次测量正在执行时拒绝修改，防止 TaskCompletionSource 冲突
            if (_isSingleMeasuring) return -999;

            // 获取实例级独占锁，防止并发操作
            await _hardwareLock.WaitAsync();
            try
            {
                return await Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    bool wasMeasuring = IsMeasuring;

                    // 若正在采集，必须先停止硬件循环
                    if (wasMeasuring)
                    {
                        StopMeasurement();
                        // 给予底层驱动 100ms 释放 USB 事务的缓冲时间
                        await Task.Delay(100);
                    }

                    // 更新本地内存模型
                    Config.IntegrationTimeMs = newIntegrationTime;
                    Config.AveragingCount = newAverages;

                    // 执行下发指令
                    int result = ApplyConfiguration();

                    // 若更新前为采集状态且更新成功，则自动恢复持续采集
                    if (wasMeasuring && result == AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS)
                    {
                        StartContinuousMeasurement();
                    }

                    return result;
                });
            }
            finally
            {
                _hardwareLock.Release();
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region 4. 测量模式控制 (单次 / 持续)

        /// <summary>
        /// 异步执行单次采集。
        /// </summary>
        public async Task<SpectralData> MeasureOnceAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
        {
            // 连接校验与互斥校验
            if (!Config.IsConnected || IsMeasuring || _isSingleMeasuring) return null!;

            _isSingleMeasuring = true;
            try
            {
                // 初始化异步结果通道
                _measureTcs = new TaskCompletionSource<SpectralData>();

                // 刷新参数
                ApplyConfiguration();

                // 获取非托管回调指针
                IntPtr pCallback = Marshal.GetFunctionPointerForDelegate(_callback);

                // 发起单次测量 (Count = 1)
                int result = AvantesSdk.AVS_MeasureCallback(Config.DeviceHandle, pCallback, 1);
                if (result != AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS) return null!;

                // 绑定取消令牌：支持外部强制中断等待
                using (ct.Register(() => _measureTcs.TrySetCanceled()))
                {                    
                    return await _measureTcs.Task;  // 挂起并等待回调函数通过 _measureTcs 返回结果
                }
            }
            finally
            {               
                _isSingleMeasuring = false; // 重置状态锁
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 开启硬件持续采集流。
        /// </summary>
        public void StartContinuousMeasurement()
        {
            // 防止重复开启
            if (!Config.IsConnected || IsMeasuring) return;

            IsMeasuring = true;
            _continuousCts = new CancellationTokenSource();

            // 加全局静态锁防止多台设备同时执行启停导致 SDK 崩溃
            lock (_globalSdkLock)
            {
                ApplyConfiguration();
                IntPtr pCallback = Marshal.GetFunctionPointerForDelegate(_callback);

                // 发起无限次测量 (Count = -1)
                int result = AvantesSdk.AVS_MeasureCallback(Config.DeviceHandle, pCallback, -1);

                if (result != AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS)
                {
                    IsMeasuring = false;
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 停止硬件采集循环。
        /// </summary>
        public void StopMeasurement()
        {
            if (!Config.IsConnected || !IsMeasuring) return;

            IsMeasuring = false;

            // 销毁取消令牌
            if (_continuousCts != null)
            {
                _continuousCts.Cancel();
                _continuousCts.Dispose();
                _continuousCts = null;
            }

            // 加全局静态锁保护 SDK 指令
            lock (_globalSdkLock)
            {
                AvantesSdk.AVS_StopMeasure(Config.DeviceHandle);
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region 5. 底层 C++ 回调与数据解析核心

        /// <summary>
        /// 硬件回调入口：此方法由 C++ 后台线程直接调用，严禁在此执行 UI 操作或长时间阻塞。
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="handle">触发事件的设备句柄。</param>
        /// <param name="status">执行状态码 (0 为 ERR_SUCCESS)。</param>
        private void OnMeasurementCompleted(ref int handle, ref int status)
        {
            // 路由 A：单次测量模式逻辑
            if (_measureTcs != null && !_measureTcs.Task.IsCompleted)
            {
                ProcessAndSetTcs(handle, status);
                return;
            }

            // 路由 B：持续测量模式逻辑 (需校验运行状态)
            if (!IsMeasuring) return;

            if (status == AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS)
            {
                // 从非托管内存提取数据
                var data = FetchSpectralData(handle);
                if (data != null)
                {
                    // 通过事件向业务层推送数据包
                    DataReady?.Invoke(data);
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 从 SDK 缓冲区提取波长映射表与光强计数表。
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="handle">硬件句柄。</param>
        /// <returns>封装好的光谱实体模型。</returns>
        private SpectralData? FetchSpectralData(int handle)
        {
            try
            {
                uint timestamp = 0;

                // 声明接收非托管数组的托管封装结构体
                var wavelengths = new AvantesSdk.PixelArrayType();
                var intensities = new AvantesSdk.PixelArrayType();

                // 提取波长校准数组与原始 Scope 数据
                int resLambda = AvantesSdk.AVS_GetLambda(handle, ref wavelengths);
                int resData = AvantesSdk.AVS_GetScopeData(handle, ref timestamp, ref intensities);

                // 仅当两个底层接口均成功返回时才进行数据裁剪
                if (resLambda == AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS && resData == AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS)
                {
                    // 根据传感器有效像素边界进行裁剪 (防止读取末尾的无效内存位 0 )
                    int validLength = Config.StopPixel + 1;

                    double[] validWavelengths = wavelengths.Value.Take(validLength).ToArray();
                    double[] validIntensities = intensities.Value.Take(validLength).ToArray();

                    // 实例化 DTO 实体返回
                    return new SpectralData(validWavelengths, validIntensities, Config.SerialNumber);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            { }

            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 单次测量专用处理：提取数据并终结挂起的异步任务隧道。
        /// </summary>
        private void ProcessAndSetTcs(int handle, int status)
        {
            // 状态异常时，向 Task 注入 null 防止外部 await 永久挂起
            if (status != AvantesSdk.ERR_SUCCESS)
            {
                _measureTcs?.TrySetResult(null!);
                return;
            }

            var data = FetchSpectralData(handle);
            _measureTcs?.TrySetResult(data!);
        }

        #endregion

        #region 6. 资源释放逻辑

        /// <summary>
        /// 释放硬件资源。包含停止采集、反激活句柄及注销事件订阅。
        /// </summary>
        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (_isDisposed) return;
         
            StopMeasurement();  // 物理停机

            // 释放 SDK 占用的 USB 资源句柄
            if (Config.DeviceHandle != -1)
            {
                AvantesSdk.AVS_Deactivate(Config.DeviceHandle);
            }

            // 断开消息链
            DataReady = null;
            _isDisposed = true;
        }

        #endregion
    }
}

using GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.Spectrometer;

/*
 * 鏂囦欢鍚? SpectrometerLogic.cs
 * 鎻忚堪: 鍏夎氨鏁版嵁绠楁硶涓績銆傛彁渚涚函鏁板璁＄畻鏀寔锛屽寘鎷璁惧鍏夎氨鎷兼帴銆佺‖浠堕ケ鍜屽害鏍￠獙鍙婂姩鎬佸宄扮畻娉曘€?
 * 鏈被閲囩敤闈欐€佸唴瀛樻睜璁捐锛屾牳蹇冩嫾鎺ョ畻娉曞疄鐜颁簡闆跺唴瀛樺垎閰嶏紝浠ヨ閬块珮棰戦噰闆嗗満鏅笅鐨?GC 鎶栧姩銆?

 * 2. 淇敼 _maxBufferSize 鏃堕渶纭繚棰勭暀瓒冲鐨勯潤鎬佺┖闂翠互瀹圭撼鎵€鏈夊湪绾胯澶囩殑鍍忕礌鎬诲拰銆?

namespace GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Services.Spectrometer.Logic
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 鍏夎氨绠楁硶閫昏緫绫伙細鍖呭惈鎷兼帴銆佸宄般€佹牎楠岀瓑绾暟瀛﹁绠楅€昏緫銆?
    /// </summary>
    public static class SpectrometerLogic
    {
        #region 1. 鍏ㄥ眬闈欐€佸唴瀛樻睜

        /// <summary>
        /// 闈欐€佺紦鍐插尯鍒濆瀹归噺銆傛敮鎸?4 鍙?4096 鍍忕礌璁惧鐨勫叏閲忔嫾鎺ラ渶姹傘€?
        /// </summary>
        private static int _maxBufferSize = 16384;

        /// <summary>
        /// 娉㈤暱闈欐€佸唴瀛樻睜锛岀敤浜庡師鍦版帓搴忎笌鍚堝苟銆?
        /// </summary>
        private static double[] _bufferWavelengths = new double[_maxBufferSize];

        /// <summary>
        /// 寮哄害闈欐€佸唴瀛樻睜锛屼笌娉㈤暱姹犲悓姝ョЩ鍔ㄣ€?
        /// </summary>
        private static double[] _bufferIntensities = new double[_maxBufferSize];

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎷兼帴绠楁硶涓撳睘骞跺彂閿侊紝闃叉澶氬彴绠＄悊绫诲悓鏃惰皟鐢ㄥ鑷寸紦鍐插尯姹℃煋銆?
        /// </summary>
        private static readonly object _stitchLock = new object();

        #endregion

        #region 2. 澶氭満鍏夎氨鎷兼帴绠楁硶 (Stitching Algorithm)

        public static SpectralData? PerformStitching(IEnumerable<SpectralData> dataCollection, double mergeTolerance = 0.01)
        {
            if (dataCollection == null) return null;

            lock (_stitchLock)
            {
                int totalLength = 0;
                int deviceCount = 0;

                // 棰勬壂鎻忥細纭畾鏈鎷兼帴鎵€闇€鐨勬€诲師濮嬪儚绱犻噺
                foreach (var data in dataCollection)
                {
                    if (data.Wavelengths != null) totalLength += data.Wavelengths.Length;
                    deviceCount++;
                }

                if (deviceCount < 2 || totalLength == 0) return null;

                // 鑻ュ綋鍓嶇紦鍐插尯涓嶈冻浠ュ绾冲叏閲忔暟鎹紝鎵ц鍊嶅鎵╁
                if (totalLength > _bufferWavelengths.Length)
                {
                    _maxBufferSize = totalLength * 2;
                    _bufferWavelengths = new double[_maxBufferSize];
                    _bufferIntensities = new double[_maxBufferSize];
                }

                // --- 姝ラ 1: 鏁版嵁绾挎€у钩閾?---
                int currentOffset = 0;
                foreach (var data in dataCollection)
                {
                    if (data.Wavelengths != null && data.Intensities != null)
                    {
                        int len = Math.Min(data.Wavelengths.Length, data.Intensities.Length);
                        Array.Copy(data.Wavelengths, 0, _bufferWavelengths, currentOffset, len);
                        Array.Copy(data.Intensities, 0, _bufferIntensities, currentOffset, len);
                        currentOffset += len;
                    }
                }

                // --- 姝ラ 2: 鐗╃悊娉㈤暱鎺掑簭 ---              
                Array.Sort(_bufferWavelengths, _bufferIntensities, 0, totalLength); // 鍩轰簬娉㈤暱姹犲寮哄害姹犺繘琛岃仈鍔ㄥ崌搴忔帓鍒?

                // 鎷兼帴娈垫尝闀垮榻愭牎姝ｏ紙娑堥櫎璁惧闂存尝闀垮井宸級
                for (int i = 1; i < totalLength; i++)
                {
                    double prev = _bufferWavelengths[i - 1];
                    double curr = _bufferWavelengths[i];
                    // 鐩搁偦娉㈤暱寮傚父璺冲彉>1nm鍒ゅ畾涓烘嫾鎺ョ紳锛屽己鍒跺钩婊?
                    if (curr - prev > 1.0)
                    {
                        _bufferWavelengths[i] = prev + 0.1;
                    }
                }

                // --- 姝ラ 3: 宄板€间繚鎶ゅ幓閲?---
                int validCount = 0; // 鎱㈡寚閽堬細鎸囧悜宸插鐞嗗ソ鐨勬湁鏁堟暟鎹湯灏?

                // 蹇寚閽?i 鍚戝悗鎵弿锛屽鎵鹃噸鍙犲尯闂?
                for (int i = 1; i < totalLength; i++)
                {
                    // 鑻ュ綋鍓嶇偣涓庝笂涓€涓湁鏁堢偣璺濈灏忎簬瀹瑰樊锛屽垯瑙嗕负鐗╃悊閲嶅彔
                    if (Math.Abs(_bufferWavelengths[i] - _bufferWavelengths[validCount]) <= mergeTolerance)
                    {
                        // 鎵ц宄板€间繚鎸侊細閲嶅悎鍖哄煙浠呬繚鐣欒兘閲忔渶楂樼殑鐐癸紝涓㈠純浣庡己搴﹀櫔澹扮偣
                        if (_bufferIntensities[i] > _bufferIntensities[validCount])
                        {
                            _bufferWavelengths[validCount] = _bufferWavelengths[i];
                            _bufferIntensities[validCount] = _bufferIntensities[i];
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        // 璺濈瓒冲锛屽垽瀹氫负鐙珛娉㈤暱鐐癸紝鎱㈡寚閽堟杩?
                        validCount++;
                        _bufferWavelengths[validCount] = _bufferWavelengths[i];
                        _bufferIntensities[validCount] = _bufferIntensities[i];
                    }
                }

                // 鏈€缁堝悎骞跺悗鐨勬湁鏁堢偣鏁?
                int finalLength = validCount + 1;

                // --- 姝ラ 4: 缁撴灉杈撳嚭 (瀹炰緥鍖? ---
                double[] finalW = new double[finalLength];
                double[] finalI = new double[finalLength];
                // 閫愬厓绱犺祴鍊硷紝閬垮厤Array.Copy娴偣灏惧樊
                for (int i = 0; i < finalLength; i++)
                {
                    finalW[i] = _bufferWavelengths[i];
                    finalI[i] = _bufferIntensities[i];
                }

                return new SpectralData(finalW, finalI, "Combined_System");
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region 3. 鍗曟簮鏁版嵁鍒嗘瀽涓庢牎楠?

        /// <summary>
        /// 纭欢杩囨洕鏍￠獙銆?
        /// 鍩轰簬 16-bit ADC 鐞嗚涓婇檺 65535锛岃瀹?65000 涓轰笟鍔″畨鍏ㄩ璀︾嚎銆?
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="data">寰呮牎楠岀殑鍏夎氨甯с€?/param>
        /// <returns>鑻ヤ换鎰忓儚绱犺秴杩?65000 Counts 鍒欒繑鍥?True銆?/returns>
        public static bool CheckSaturation(SpectralData data)
        {
            if (data?.Intensities == null || data.Intensities.Length == 0) return false;
            // 浣跨敤 Any 蹇€熸煡鎵捐繃鏇濈偣
            return data.Intensities.Any(i => i > 65000);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍏ㄥ眬鏈€楂樺嘲瀵讳紭銆?
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="data">鍏夎氨鏁版嵁甯с€?/param>
        /// <returns>杩斿洖鍖呭惈 (娉㈤暱, 鏈€澶у己搴? 鐨勫厓缁勩€?/returns>
        public static (double wavelength, double intensity) FindPeak(SpectralData data)
        {
            if (data?.Intensities == null || data.Intensities.Length == 0) return (0, 0);

            double maxIntensity = data.Intensities.Max();
            int index = Array.IndexOf(data.Intensities, maxIntensity);
            double wavelength = data.Wavelengths[index];

            return (wavelength, maxIntensity);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 灞€閮ㄥ姩鎬佸宄?(闃茶烦宄扮畻娉?銆?
        /// 鍦ㄧ洰鏍囨尝闀跨獥鍙ｅ唴閫氳繃鈥滃眬閮ㄥ北澶存娴嬧€濆鎵剧湡瀹炴渶楂樼偣銆傝嫢瀛樺湪澶氫釜灞€閮ㄦ瀬澶у€硷紝鍒欎紭鍏堥€夋嫨鐗╃悊浣嶇疆鏈€鎺ヨ繎鐩爣鍊肩殑灞卞ご銆?
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="data">褰撳墠鍏夎氨甯с€?/param>
        /// <param name="targetWavelength">鐞嗚涓績娉㈤暱 (閫氬父鏉ヨ嚜 UI 浜や簰鐐瑰嚮)銆?/param>
        /// <param name="tolerance">鎼滅储瀹瑰樊甯﹀ (卤 nm)銆?/param>
        /// <returns>瀵绘壘鍒扮殑瀹為檯鐗╃悊鐗瑰緛宄版尝闀裤€?/returns>
        public static double GetActualPeakWavelength(SpectralData data, double targetWavelength, double tolerance)
        {
            if (data?.Wavelengths == null || data.Wavelengths.Length == 0) return targetWavelength;

            int bestPeakIndex = -1;
            double minDistanceToTarget = double.MaxValue;
            double absoluteMaxIntensity = -1;
            int absoluteMaxIndex = -1;

            // 1. 閬嶅巻瀵绘壘绐楀彛鍐呯殑鎵€鏈夆€滃眬閮ㄦ瀬澶у€硷紙灞卞ご锛夆€?
            for (int i = 1; i < data.Wavelengths.Length - 1; i++)
            {
                double currentW = data.Wavelengths[i];

                if (Math.Abs(currentW - targetWavelength) <= tolerance)
                {
                    double currentI = data.Intensities[i];
                    double prevI = data.Intensities[i - 1];
                    double nextI = data.Intensities[i + 1];

                    // 璁板綍绐楀彛鍐呯殑缁濆鏈€澶у€硷紝浣滀负澶囩敤闃查€€鍖栨柟妗?
                    if (currentI > absoluteMaxIntensity)
                    {
                        absoluteMaxIntensity = currentI;
                        absoluteMaxIndex = i;
                    }

                    // 鏍稿績閫昏緫锛氬垽鏂槸鍚︿负灞€閮ㄦ瀬澶у€?(灞卞ご)
                    if (currentI > prevI && currentI > nextI)
                    {
                        // 璁＄畻璇ュ北澶翠笌鐩爣娉㈤暱鐨勭墿鐞嗚窛绂?
                        double distance = Math.Abs(currentW - targetWavelength);
                        
                        // 璺濈浼樺厛鍘熷垯锛氶€夋嫨璺濈鐩爣娉㈤暱鏈€杩戠殑灞卞ご
                        if (distance < minDistanceToTarget)
                        {
                            minDistanceToTarget = distance;
                            bestPeakIndex = i;
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 鎬ц兘浼樺寲锛氬洜涓烘尝闀挎槸鍗囧簭鐨勶紝瓒呰繃绐楀彛涓婇檺鍗冲彲鍋滄寰幆
                if (currentW > targetWavelength + tolerance) break;
            }

            // 2. 缁撴灉瑁佸畾
            // 浼樺厛杩斿洖璺濈鏈€杩戠殑鐪熷疄灞€閮ㄥ北澶?
            if (bestPeakIndex != -1)
            {
                return data.Wavelengths[bestPeakIndex];
            }
            // 闃查€€鍖栧鐞嗭細濡傛灉绐楀彛鍐呮壘涓嶅埌浠讳綍鈥滃北澶粹€濓紙渚嬪钀藉湪涓€娈靛钩缂撲笅鍧′笂锛夛紝鍒欓€€鍖栦负鎵剧粷瀵规渶澶у€?
            else if (absoluteMaxIndex != -1)
            {
                return data.Wavelengths[absoluteMaxIndex];
            }

            return targetWavelength;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 瀹氱偣鍏夊己鏄犲皠銆?
        /// 瀵绘壘鍏夎氨涓笌鎸囧畾鐞嗚娉㈤暱鍦ㄧ墿鐞嗕笂鏈€鎺ヨ繎鐨勫儚绱犵偣寮哄害銆?
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="data">鍏夎氨鏁版嵁銆?/param>
        /// <param name="targetWavelength">鐩爣娉㈤暱鍧愭爣銆?/param>
        /// <returns>瀵瑰簲鐨勫厜寮鸿鏁?(Counts)銆?/returns>
        public static double GetIntensityAtWavelength(SpectralData data, double targetWavelength)
        {
            if (data?.Wavelengths == null || data.Wavelengths.Length == 0) return 0;

            int bestIndex = 0;
            double minDiff = double.MaxValue;

            // 绠€鍗曠殑鏈€鎺ヨ繎鎼滅储
            for (int i = 0; i < data.Wavelengths.Length; i++)
            {
                double diff = Math.Abs(data.Wavelengths[i] - targetWavelength);
                if (diff < minDiff)
                {
                    minDiff = diff;
                    bestIndex = i;
                }
            }
            return data.Intensities[bestIndex];
        }

        #endregion
    }
}

using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Messaging;
using GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models;
using GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.Messages;
using GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Services;
using System.Windows;
using static GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.AppConfig;

namespace GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels
{
    public partial class AnalysisWorkstationViewModel : ObservableObject
    {
        public SampleMeasurementViewModel ContinuousVM { get; }
        public FlowInjectionViewModel FlowInjectionVM { get; }

        // ================= 核心：必须使用 ObservableProperty 让 UI 知道值变了 =================
        [ObservableProperty]
        private Visibility _continuousVisibility = Visibility.Visible;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private Visibility _flowInjectionVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;

        public AnalysisWorkstationViewModel(
            SampleMeasurementViewModel continuousVM,
            FlowInjectionViewModel flowInjectionVM,
            JsonConfigService configService)
        {
            ContinuousVM = continuousVM;
            FlowInjectionVM = flowInjectionVM;

            // 1. 初始化时读取配置并设定初始界面
            var config = configService.Load();
            SwitchMode(config.CurrentMeasurementMode);

            // 2. 监听仪表台传来的切换消息
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<MeasurementModeChangedMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                // 必须在 UI 线程执行切换，否则 Visibility 改变无效
                Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                {
                    SwitchMode(m.Value);
                });
            });
        }

        private void SwitchMode(MeasurementMode mode)
        {
            // 直接赋值 Visibility 枚举，这是最稳妥的 WPF 显隐控制方式
            if (mode == MeasurementMode.Continuous)
            {
                ContinuousVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
                FlowInjectionVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
            }
            else
            {
                ContinuousVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
                FlowInjectionVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
            }
        }
    }
}

using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Services;
using System.Windows;

/*
 * 文件名: BatteryViewModel.cs
 * 描述: 电池视图模型类，负责将底层 BatteryService 的实时状态（电量、在线状态、充电工况）转换为 UI 可绑定的属性。
 * 本类通过订阅服务的事件实现被动更新，并利用 Dispatcher 确保跨线程 UI 刷新的安全性。
 * 维护指南: 
 * 1. 属性变更通知依赖于 ObservableProperty 生成的 partial 成员。
 * 2. 状态同步逻辑集中在 SyncStatus 方法中，修改物理量映射时需同步检查该方法。
 */

namespace GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 电池状态视图模型：连接硬件服务与前端页面的电量显示组件。
    /// </summary>
    public partial class BatteryViewModel : ObservableObject
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// 关联的电池业务服务引用。
        /// </summary>
        private readonly BatteryService _batteryService;

        /// <summary>
        /// 电池剩余电量百分比 (0-100)。对应 UI 进度条或数值显示。
        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        private int _percentage;

        /// <summary>
        /// 电池通讯在线状态标识。用于控制 UI 离线图标的可见性。
        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        private bool _isOnline;

        /// <summary>
        /// 充电状态标识。True 表示正在充电，False 表示放电或静置。
        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        private bool _isCharging;

        /// <summary>
        /// 构造函数：注入服务实例，挂载事件监听并启动监控。
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="batteryService">电池业务服务实例。</param>
        public BatteryViewModel(BatteryService batteryService)
        {
            _batteryService = batteryService;

            // 订阅底层服务的状态变更通知
            _batteryService.StatusUpdated += OnStatusUpdated;

            // 执行首次数据同步
            SyncStatus();

            // 激活服务内部的定时查询逻辑
            _batteryService.Start();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 响应服务层的状态更新事件。
        /// 职责：将后台线程触发的事件调度至主线程执行属性赋值。
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="sender">事件源。</param>
        /// <param name="e">事件参数。</param>
        private void OnStatusUpdated(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            // 利用 WPF Dispatcher 确保在 UI 线程执行 SyncStatus，防止跨线程操作异常
            Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(SyncStatus);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 核心同步逻辑：从服务层抓取最新物理状态并赋值给本地 Observable 属性。
        /// </summary>
        private void SyncStatus()
        {
            Percentage = _batteryService.Percentage;
            IsOnline = _batteryService.IsOnline;
            IsCharging = _batteryService.IsCharging;
        }
    }
}

        private const int MaxReigniteAttempts = 5;

        #endregion

        #region 5. 鏋勯€犲嚱鏁颁笌鍒濆鍖栫绾?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鏋勯€?ControlPanelViewModel 骞舵墽琛屽瓙妯″潡娉ㄥ叆銆?        /// </summary>
        public ControlPanelViewModel(GeneralDeviceService generalDeviceService, JsonConfigService jsonConfigService, BatteryViewModel batteryVM,
            HighVoltageViewModel highVoltageVM, PeristalticPumpViewModel peristalticPumpVM, SyringePumpViewModel syringePumpVM)
        {
            _generalDeviceService = generalDeviceService;
            _jsonConfigService = jsonConfigService;
            _batteryVM = batteryVM;
            _hvVM = highVoltageVM;
            _pumpVM = peristalticPumpVM;
            _syringeVM = syringePumpVM;

            // 鍒濆鍖栧厜璋变华鍙婂瓙鍗＄墖闆嗗悎
            InitSubModules();
            // 鎸傝浇纭欢杩愯鐘舵€佺洃鎺?            SubscribeHardwareEvents();
            // 娉ㄥ唽鍏ㄥ眬娑堟伅澶勭悊
            RegisterGlobalMessages();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍒濆鍖栧厜璋变华鍖呰鍣紝骞堕厤缃弬鏁版寔涔呭寲濮旀墭銆?        /// </summary>
        private void InitSubModules()
        {
            var appConfig = _jsonConfigService.Load();
            
            // 鍚屾閰嶇疆鏂囦欢涓殑鍒濆娴嬫牱妯″紡鐘舵€?            IsFlowInjectionMode = appConfig.CurrentMeasurementMode == MeasurementMode.FlowInjection;

            var specConfig = new SpectrometerConfig();

            if (appConfig.LastIntegrationTime > 0) specConfig.IntegrationTimeMs = appConfig.LastIntegrationTime;
            if (appConfig.LastAveragingCount > 0) specConfig.AveragingCount = appConfig.LastAveragingCount;

            // 鍒濆鍖栧厜璋变华鍖呰鍣?            SpecVM = new SpectrometerViewModel(specConfig, null!)
            {
                SaveConfigAction = () =>
                {
                    var config = _jsonConfigService.Load();
                    config.LastIntegrationTime = SpecVM.IntegrationTimeMs;
                    config.LastAveragingCount = SpecVM.AveragingCount;
                    _jsonConfigService.Save(config);
                }
            };

            // 缁勮纭欢鍗＄墖灞曠ず鍒楄〃
            Devices.Add(_hvVM);
            Devices.Add(_pumpVM);
            Devices.Add(_syringeVM);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 璁㈤槄鐢垫簮涓庢车鐨勫叧閿睘鎬у彉鍖栥€?        /// </summary>
        private void SubscribeHardwareEvents()
        {
            _hvVM.PropertyChanged += OnHardwareStateChanged;
            _pumpVM.PropertyChanged += OnHardwareStateChanged;

            // 鐩戝惉鍏夎氨浠‖浠舵睜鍙樺寲
            Services.Spectrometer.SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices.CollectionChanged += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (Services.Spectrometer.SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices.Count == 0 && SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring)
                {
                    Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                    {
                        // 鏂紑鍚庤嚜鍔ㄦ仮澶嶅仠姝㈢姸鎬?                        SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring = false;
                        SpecVM.Model.IsConnected = false;
                        SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber = "宸叉柇寮€杩炴帴";
                        SpecVM.UpdateFromModel();
                        StatusInfo = "鏈娴嬪埌鍏夎氨浠?;
                        _isToggling = false;

                        // 寮哄埗娓呯┖娈嬩綑娉㈠舰
                        WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new ClearWaveformMessage());
                    });
                }
            };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 娉ㄥ唽鍏ㄥ眬寮卞紩鐢ㄦ秷鎭紝澶勭悊鍏夎氨纭欢杩炴帴鍙婂厜璋辨暟鎹祦銆?        /// </summary>
        private void RegisterGlobalMessages()
        {
            // 鍝嶅簲鍏夎氨浠‖浠剁殑鍏ㄥ眬绂佺敤/鍚敤
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SpectrometerStatusMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                var incomingConfig = m.Value;
                if (incomingConfig.SerialNumber == "GLOBAL_SWITCH" && !incomingConfig.IsConnected)
                {
                    Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                    {
                        SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring = false;
                        SpecVM.Model.IsConnected = false;
                        SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber = "宸叉柇寮€杩炴帴";
                        SpecVM.UpdateFromModel();
                        StatusInfo = "鍏夎氨浠‖浠跺凡琚叏灞€绂佺敤";
                        _isToggling = false;
                    });
                }
            });

            // 澶勭悊瀹炴椂鍏夎氨鏁版嵁娴佸叆锛屽苟鎵ц娉㈤暱涓€鑷存€т繚鎶ゆ牎楠?            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SpectralDataMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                _latestSpectralData = m.Value;

                if (IsReferenceFileLoaded && _loadedReferenceData != null)
                {
                    double[] liveX = _latestSpectralData.Wavelengths;
                    double[] fileX = _loadedReferenceData.Wavelengths;

                    // 鑻ョ‖浠舵尝闀胯寖鍥翠笌鍙傝€冩枃浠跺啿绐侊紝寮哄埗绉婚櫎鍙傝€冨眰浠ラ槻鏄剧ず鐣稿彉
                    if (liveX.Length != fileX.Length || Math.Abs(liveX[0] - fileX[0]) > 0.1 || Math.Abs(liveX[^1] - fileX[^1]) > 0.1)
                    {
                        _loadedReferenceData = null;
                        Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                        {
                            IsReferenceFileLoaded = false;
                            StatusInfo = "娉㈤暱涓嶅尮閰嶏紝宸茶嚜鍔ㄥ叧闂弬鑰冨厜璋?;
                        });
                        WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new ClearReferencePlotMessage());
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        #endregion

        #region 6. 鏍稿績涓氬姟娴佹按绾?(鍚仠璋冨害 & 鎸佷箙鍖?

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍒囨崲鍏夎氨閲囬泦鐘舵€併€傛墽琛岃澶囨壂鎻忋€佸垵濮嬪寲鍙婇厤缃笅鍙戝簭鍒椼€?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ToggleSpectrometer()
        {
            var config = _jsonConfigService.Load();
            if (!config.IsSpectrometerEnabled)
            {
                StatusInfo = "璇峰厛鍦ㄨ缃腑鍚敤鍏夎氨浠繛鎺?;
                return;
            }

            if (_isToggling) return;
            _isToggling = true;

            try
            {
                // 棣栨杩炴帴闇€鎵ц鍏ㄦ€荤嚎璁惧鎺㈡祴
                if (SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices.Count == 0)
                {
                    StatusInfo = "姝ｅ湪鎵弿鍏夎氨浠?..";
                    int count = await SpectrometerManager.Instance.DiscoverAndInitDevicesAsync();
                    if (count == 0) 
                    { 
                        StatusInfo = "鏈娴嬪埌鍏夎氨浠?;
                        WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new ClearWaveformMessage());
                        return; 
                    }
                }

                var devices = SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices;
                int deviceCount = devices.Count;

                if (!SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring)
                {
                    StatusInfo = "姝ｅ湪鍒濆鍖栫‖浠?..";
                    bool allSuccess = await Task.Run(async () =>
                    {
                        bool success = true;
                        foreach (var service in devices)
                        {
                            bool isInitialized = service.Config.IsConnected || await service.InitializeAsync();
                            if (isInitialized)
                                await service.UpdateConfigurationAsync(SpecVM.IntegrationTimeMs, SpecVM.AveragingCount);
                            else
                            {
                                success = false;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        if (success) SpectrometerManager.Instance.StartAll();
                        return success;
                    });

                    if (allSuccess && devices.Any())
                    {
                        var firstDevice = devices.First();
                        SpecVM.SetService(firstDevice);
                        SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber = devices.Count == 1 ? firstDevice.Config.SerialNumber : "鑱旀満妯″紡";
                        SpecVM.Model.IsConnected = true;
                        SpecVM.UpdateFromModel();
                        SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring = true;
                        StatusInfo = devices.Count == 1 ? $"閲囬泦杩涜涓? {firstDevice.Config.SerialNumber}" : $"澶氭満鑱旀満閲囬泦杩愯涓?(鍏?{devices.Count} 鍙?";
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        StatusInfo = devices.Any() ? "閮ㄥ垎璁惧鍒濆鍖栧け璐? : "纭欢杩炴帴鍦ㄥ惎鍔ㄦ椂鏂紑";
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    StatusInfo = "姝ｅ湪瀹夊叏鍋滄纭欢...";
                    await Task.Run(() => SpectrometerManager.Instance.StopAll());
                    SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring = false;
                    StatusInfo = "閲囬泦宸插仠姝?;
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                _isToggling = false;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鑷姩鍖栬剼鏈繚瀛樺紩鎿庛€傛帶鍒跺悗鍙扮嚎绋嬪畾鏃舵姄鍙栨渶鏂板厜璋卞抚骞舵殏瀛樿嚦鍐呭瓨 List銆?        /// </summary>
        private void ToggleScriptSaveAsync()
        {
            if (IsScriptSaving)
            {
                _scriptSaveCts?.Cancel();
                IsScriptSaving = false;
                StatusInfo = "姝ｅ湪涓閲囬泦骞朵繚瀛樺凡缂撳瓨鏁版嵁...";
                return;
            }

            if (!SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring || _latestSpectralData == null)
            {
                StatusInfo = "璇峰厛鍚姩鍏夎氨浠噰闆嗘暟鎹?;
                return;
            }

            var config = _jsonConfigService.Load();
            int count = config.ScriptSaveCount;
            int interval = config.ScriptSaveInterval;
            string dirPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.ScriptSaveDirectory) || !System.IO.Directory.Exists(config.ScriptSaveDirectory)
                ? Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop) : config.ScriptSaveDirectory;

            string serial = SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber == "宸叉柇寮€杩炴帴" ? "Unknown" : (SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber == "鑱旀満妯″紡" ? "MultiDevice" : SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber);
            string fileName = $"Spectrum_Auto_{serial}_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}.xlsx";
            string fullPath = System.IO.Path.Combine(dirPath, fileName);

            IsScriptSaving = true;
            _isEmergencyShutdown = false;
            _scriptSaveCts = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var token = _scriptSaveCts.Token;

            float currentIntTime = SpecVM.IntegrationTimeMs;
            uint currentAvgCount = SpecVM.AveragingCount;

            _ = Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                _scriptSaveCache.Clear();
                try
                {
                    for (int i = 1; i <= count; i++)
                    {
                        token.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                        Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => StatusInfo = $"鑴氭湰杩愯涓? 姝ｅ湪缂撳瓨 {i}/{count} ...");

                        var dataToSave = _latestSpectralData;
                        if (dataToSave != null && dataToSave.Wavelengths != null)
                        {
                            // 鎵ц娣卞害鎷疯礉瀛樺叆鍐呭瓨姹狅紝闃叉鍚庣画寮曠敤琚‖浠舵洿鏂拌鐩?                            _scriptSaveCache.Add(new SpectralData
                            {
                                Wavelengths = dataToSave.Wavelengths.ToArray(),
                                Intensities = dataToSave.Intensities.ToArray(),
                                AcquisitionTime = dataToSave.AcquisitionTime
                            });
                        }
                        if (i < count) await Task.Delay(interval * 1000, token);
                    }
                }
                catch (OperationCanceledException) { }
                catch (Exception) { Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => StatusInfo = "缂撳瓨鏁版嵁鏃跺彂鐢熷紓甯?); }
                finally
                {
                    // 涓氬姟鏀跺熬锛氭墽琛屾壒閲忕墿鐞嗗啓鍏?                    if (!_isEmergencyShutdown)
                    {
                        if (_scriptSaveCache.Count > 0)
                        {
                            Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => StatusInfo = $"姝ｅ湪灏?{_scriptSaveCache.Count} 甯ф暟鎹啓鍏ョ‖鐩橈紝璇风◢鍊?..");
                            using var exportService = new ExcelExportService();
                            var dataToSave = _scriptSaveCache.ToList();
                            bool success = exportService.ExportScriptSpectraBulkAsync(fullPath, dataToSave, currentIntTime, currentAvgCount).GetAwaiter().GetResult();

                            Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                            {
                                StatusInfo = success ? $"鑴氭湰淇濆瓨瀹屾垚 (鍏?{dataToSave.Count} 甯э紝宸插瓨鑷?{fileName})" : "鍐欏叆纭洏澶辫触锛岃妫€鏌ユ枃浠舵槸鍚﹁鍗犵敤";
                                IsScriptSaving = false;
                            });
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => { StatusInfo = "鏈噰闆嗗埌鏈夋晥鏁版嵁锛屽凡鍙栨秷淇濆瓨"; IsScriptSaving = false; });
                        }
                        _scriptSaveCache.Clear();
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 褰撹蒋浠舵剰澶栧叧闂椂锛岀珛鍗崇粓姝㈡墍鏈変换鍔″苟寮哄埗灏嗗唴瀛樻睜鏁版嵁鍐欏叆妗岄潰 Exit 鏂囦欢銆?        /// </summary>
        public void EmergencySave()
        {
            if (!IsScriptSaving || _scriptSaveCache.Count == 0) return;

            _isEmergencyShutdown = true;
            IsScriptSaving = false;
            _scriptSaveCts?.Cancel();

            var config = _jsonConfigService.Load();
            string dirPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.ScriptSaveDirectory) ? Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop) : config.ScriptSaveDirectory;
            string serial = SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber == "鑱旀満妯″紡" ? "MultiDevice" : (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber) ? "Unknown" : SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber);
            string fileName = $"Spectrum_Auto_{serial}_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}_Exit.xlsx";

            using var exportService = new ExcelExportService();
            exportService.ExportScriptSpectraBulkAsync(System.IO.Path.Combine(dirPath, fileName), _scriptSaveCache.ToList(), SpecVM.IntegrationTimeMs, SpecVM.AveragingCount).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
        }

        #endregion

        #region 7. UI 鎺у埗涓庨€氱敤瀵硅瘽妗嗗懡浠?(Commands)

        /// <summary> 鎷︽埅杞悜闃€ UI 寮€鍏筹紝鍚屾涓嬪彂鑷崇墿鐞?IO 瀵勫瓨鍣ㄣ€?</summary>
        partial void OnIsSteeringValveActiveChanged(bool value) => _generalDeviceService.ControlSteeringValve(value);

        /// <summary> 鎵ц鍗曟鐐圭伀鑴夊啿鍛戒护銆?</summary>
        [RelayCommand] private void Fire() => _generalDeviceService.Fire();

        /// <summary> 鍙戦€佸叏灞€娑堟伅璇锋眰鍥捐〃瑙嗙獥鑷€傚簲缂╂斁銆?</summary>
        [RelayCommand] private void AutoRange() => WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new AutoRangeRequestMessage());

        /// <summary> 鍙戦€佸叏灞€娑堟伅璇锋眰鎭㈠鍥捐〃鏈€澶ф瀬闄愯閲庛€?</summary>
        [RelayCommand] private void MaxRange() => WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new MaxRangeRequestMessage());

        /// <summary> 鍚姩涓夌淮骞冲彴鎺у埗涓庤嚜鍔ㄦ牎鍑嗗璇濇銆?</summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenPlatform3DWindow()
        {
            var platformService = App.Services.GetRequiredService<IPlatform3DService>();
            var calibrationService = App.Services.GetRequiredService<PlatformCalibrationService>();
            var jsonConfigService = App.Services.GetRequiredService<JsonConfigService>();
            var peakTrackingService = App.Services.GetRequiredService<PeakTrackingService>();

            var window = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Views.Dialogs.Platform3DWindow();
            var vm = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels.Dialogs.Platform3DViewModel(
                platformService, calibrationService, jsonConfigService, peakTrackingService, () => window.Close());

            window.DataContext = vm;
            window.WindowStartupLocation = System.Windows.WindowStartupLocation.CenterScreen;

            // 鑾峰彇褰撳墠涓荤獥鍙?            var mainWindow = System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow;

            // 瀹氫箟涓荤獥鍙ｅ叧闂椂鐨勮仈鍔ㄥ姩浣滐細鍏虫帀褰撳墠鐨勪笁缁村钩鍙扮獥鍙?            EventHandler mainWindowClosedHandler = (sender, e) =>
            {
                window.Close();
            };

            if (mainWindow != null)
            {
                // 缁戝畾涓荤獥鍙ｅ叧闂簨浠?                mainWindow.Closed += mainWindowClosedHandler;
            }

            // 璁㈤槄绐楀彛鑷韩鐨勫叧闂簨浠?            window.Closed += (sender, e) =>
            {               

                // 濡傛灉瀛愮獥鍙ｈ嚜宸卞厛琚叧闂簡锛屽繀椤昏В缁戜富绐楀彛鐨勪簨浠讹紝闃叉鍐呭瓨娉勬紡
                if (mainWindow != null)
                {
                    mainWindow.Closed -= mainWindowClosedHandler;
                }
            };

            vm.SavePreferences();
            window.Show();
        }

        /// <summary> 鎵撳紑绉垎鏃堕棿璋冩暣瀵硅瘽妗嗐€?</summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenIntegrationTimeConfig()
        {
            var window = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Views.Dialogs.SpecParamSettingWindow();
            window.DataContext = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels.Dialogs.SpecParamSettingViewModel(
                "绉垎鏃堕棿璁剧疆", "鍙傛暟閰嶇疆 - 绉垎鏃堕棿 (ms)", SpecVM.IntegrationTimeMs, SpecVM.Model.MinIntegrationTime, 600000, 5.0,
                (val) => SpecVM.ChangeIntegrationTimeCommand.Execute(val), () => window.Close());
            window.Owner = Application.Current.MainWindow;
            window.ShowDialog();
        }

        /// <summary> 鎵撳紑骞冲潎娆℃暟璋冩暣瀵硅瘽妗嗐€?</summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenAveragingCountConfig()
        {
            var window = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Views.Dialogs.SpecParamSettingWindow();
            window.DataContext = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels.Dialogs.SpecParamSettingViewModel(
                "骞冲潎娆℃暟璁剧疆", "鍙傛暟閰嶇疆 - 骞冲潎娆℃暟", SpecVM.AveragingCount, 1, 10000, 1.0,
                (val) => SpecVM.ChangeAveragingCountCommand.Execute(val), () => window.Close());
            window.Owner = Application.Current.MainWindow;
            window.ShowDialog();
        }

        /// <summary> 鎵ц鍗曞抚鍏夎氨 Excel 瀵煎嚭涓氬姟銆?</summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task SingleSaveAsync()
        {
            if (_latestSpectralData?.Wavelengths == null || _latestSpectralData.Wavelengths.Length == 0)
            {
                StatusInfo = "鏆傛棤鍏夎氨鏁版嵁鍙繚瀛?;
                return;
            }
            try
            {
                StatusInfo = "姝ｅ湪瀵煎嚭鍗曞箙鍏夎氨...";
                var config = _jsonConfigService.Load();
                string dirPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.SingleSaveExportPath) || !System.IO.Directory.Exists(config.SingleSaveExportPath)
                    ? Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop) : config.SingleSaveExportPath;
                string serial = SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber == "鑱旀満妯″紡" ? "MultiDevice" : (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber) ? "Unknown" : SpecVM.Model.SerialNumber);

                using var exportService = new ExcelExportService();
                await exportService.ExportSingleSpectrumAsync(System.IO.Path.Combine(dirPath, $"Spectrum_{serial}_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}.xlsx"), _latestSpectralData.AcquisitionTime, SpecVM.IntegrationTimeMs, SpecVM.AveragingCount, _latestSpectralData.Wavelengths, _latestSpectralData.Intensities);
                StatusInfo = "鍗曞箙淇濆瓨鎴愬姛";
            }
            catch (Exception) { StatusInfo = "鍗曞箙淇濆瓨澶辫触"; }
        }

        /// <summary> 璋冨害鏈湴鍙傝€冨厜璋卞姞杞戒笟鍔°€傚寘鍚枃浠惰鍙栥€佹牸寮忔牎楠屽強娉㈤暱鍖归厤妫€鏌ャ€?</summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ToggleReferenceFileAsync()
        {
            if (IsReferenceFileLoaded)
            {
                _loadedReferenceData = null;
                IsReferenceFileLoaded = false;
                WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new ClearReferencePlotMessage());
                StatusInfo = "宸插叧闂弬鑰冨厜璋?;
            }
            else
            {
                var config = _jsonConfigService.Load();
                var dialog = new Microsoft.Win32.OpenFileDialog { Filter = "Excel Files (*.xlsx)|*.xlsx", Title = "閫夋嫨鍙傝€冨厜璋辨枃浠? };
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.SingleSaveExportPath) && System.IO.Directory.Exists(config.SingleSaveExportPath))
                    dialog.InitialDirectory = config.SingleSaveExportPath;

                if (dialog.ShowDialog() == true)
                {
                    StatusInfo = "姝ｅ湪璇诲彇鏂囦欢...";
                    using var service = new ExcelExportService();
                    var parsedData = await service.ImportSingleSpectrumAsync(dialog.FileName);

                    if (parsedData == null) { StatusInfo = "鏂囦欢璇诲彇澶辫触鎴栨牸寮忎笉绗﹀悎瑕佹眰"; return; }
                    if (SpecVM.IsCurrentlyMeasuring && _latestSpectralData?.Wavelengths != null)
                    {
                        if (_latestSpectralData.Wavelengths.Length != parsedData.Wavelengths.Length || Math.Abs(_latestSpectralData.Wavelengths[0] - parsedData.Wavelengths[0]) > 0.1)
                        {
                            StatusInfo = "鍔犺浇澶辫触锛氳鏂囦欢鐨勬尝闀胯寖鍥翠笌褰撳墠杩愯鐨勭‖浠朵笉鍖归厤";
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    _loadedReferenceData = parsedData;
                    IsReferenceFileLoaded = true;
                    WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new LoadReferencePlotMessage(_loadedReferenceData));
                    StatusInfo = $"宸插姞杞藉弬鑰冨厜璋? {parsedData.AcquisitionTime:HH:mm:ss}";
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary> 寮瑰嚭鑷姩鍖栬剼鏈繚瀛橀厤缃脊绐椼€?</summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenScriptSaveWindow()
        {
            var window = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Views.Dialogs.ScriptSaveWindow();
            window.DataContext = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels.Dialogs.ScriptSaveViewModel(_jsonConfigService, _jsonConfigService.Load(), IsScriptSaving, () => window.Close(), () => { ToggleScriptSaveAsync(); });
            window.Owner = Application.Current.MainWindow;
            window.ShowDialog();
        }

        /// <summary> 鎺ユ敹鏉ヨ嚜澶栭儴鐨勫彲鐢ㄦ樉绀洪珮搴︼紝浠ユ璁＄畻浠〃鐩樹笌鍙傛暟澶撮儴鐨勬瘮渚嬪竷灞€銆?</summary>
        public void SetInitialHeight(double height)
        {
            DashboardHeight = height;
            _originalHeight = height;
            ParamHeaderHeight = Math.Max(35, height * 0.15);
        }

        /// <summary> 骞挎挱鍚屾灞炴€э細纭繚瀛愬脊绐楄兘澶熷疄鏃舵劅搴斿悗鍙伴噰闆嗙姸鎬併€?</summary>
        partial void OnIsScriptSavingChanged(bool value) => WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new ScriptSaveStateChangedMessage(value));

        #endregion

        #region 8. 鑷姩鐐圭伀鏍稿績鎺у埗閫昏緫 (Hardware Event Callbacks)

        /// <summary> 鍝嶅簲纭欢鍏抽敭灞炴€у彉鍖栧洖璋冿紝椹卞姩绛夌瀛愪綋鐩戞帶鐘舵€佹満銆?</summary>
        private void OnHardwareStateChanged(object? sender, System.ComponentModel.PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.PropertyName == nameof(HighVoltageViewModel.MonitorCurrent) ||
                e.PropertyName == nameof(HighVoltageViewModel.IsRunning) ||
                e.PropertyName == nameof(PeristalticPumpViewModel.IsRunning))
            {
                CheckPlasmaState();
            }
        }

        /// <summary> 鏍稿績鐘舵€佺洃娴嬶細閫氳繃鐢垫祦澶у皬 (>5.0mA 涓虹ǔ锛?1.0mA 涓虹伃) 鍒ゅ畾鐕冪儳鐘舵€侊紝骞惰嚜鍔ㄨЕ鍙戝紓甯告尳鏁戙€?</summary>
        private void CheckPlasmaState()
        {
            if (!_hvVM.IsRunning || !_pumpVM.IsRunning)
            {
                _isPlasmaStable = false;
                _reigniteAttemptCount = 0;
                return;
            }

            if (double.TryParse(_hvVM.MonitorCurrent, out double current))
            {
                if (current > 5.0)
                {
                    if (!_isPlasmaStable)
                    {
                        _isPlasmaStable = true;
                        _reigniteAttemptCount = 0;
                        _isReigniting = false;
                        StatusInfo = "绛夌瀛愪綋宸茬ǔ瀹氳繍琛?;
                    }
                }
                else if (current <= 1.0 && _isPlasmaStable && !_isReigniting)
                {
                    _isPlasmaStable = false;
                    _ = HandleUnexpectedExtinctionAsync();
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary> 鎰忓鐔勭伀鎸芥晳寮傛娴佹按绾裤€傛墽琛屽欢鏃剁瓑寰呰繘娑层€侀噸澶嶇偣鐏?Fire 鍔ㄤ綔锛屽苟鍏峰纭欢鎵嬪姩涓柇鑷瘉鏈哄埗銆?</summary>
        private async Task HandleUnexpectedExtinctionAsync()
        {
            _isReigniting = true;
            if (!_jsonConfigService.Load().IsAutoReigniteEnabled)
            {
                StatusInfo = "鎰忓鐔勭伀 (鏈紑鍚嚜鍔ㄧ偣鐏?";
                _isReigniting = false;
                return;
            }

            while (_reigniteAttemptCount < MaxReigniteAttempts)
            {
                _reigniteAttemptCount++;
                StatusInfo = $"鎰忓鐔勭伀锛屽噯澶囧皾璇曠 {_reigniteAttemptCount} 娆¤嚜鍔ㄧ偣鐏?..";
                await Task.Delay(2000); // 杩涙恫缂撳啿

                if (!_hvVM.IsRunning || !_pumpVM.IsRunning)
                {
                    StatusInfo = "鑷姩鐐圭伀宸插彇娑?(纭欢宸叉墜鍔ㄥ叧闂?";
                    _isReigniting = false;
                    return;
                }

                StatusInfo = $"姝ｅ湪鎵ц绗?{_reigniteAttemptCount} 娆¤嚜鍔ㄧ偣鐏?..";
                _generalDeviceService.Fire();
                await Task.Delay(3000); // 鐐圭伀鍙嶅簲鏃堕棿

                if (double.TryParse(_hvVM.MonitorCurrent, out double current) && current > 5.0)
                {
                    _isReigniting = false;
                    return;
                }
            }

            StatusInfo = "澶氭鑷姩鐐圭伀澶辫触锛屼负淇濋殰瀹夊叏宸插垏鏂緭鍑?;
            _hvVM.IsRunning = _pumpVM.IsRunning = false;
            _isReigniting = false;
        }

        #endregion
        [ObservableProperty]
        private bool _isFlowInjectionMode;

        // 3. 瀹氫箟鍛戒护鏂规硶锛屽悕绉拌瀵归綈
        [RelayCommand]
        public void ToggleMeasurementMode()
        {
            var config = _jsonConfigService.Load();
            var mode = IsFlowInjectionMode ? MeasurementMode.FlowInjection : MeasurementMode.Continuous;
            config.CurrentMeasurementMode = mode;
            _jsonConfigService.Save(config);

            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new MeasurementModeChangedMessage(mode));
        }
    }
}

        public DataProcessingViewModel(JsonConfigService configService, ElementDatabaseService elementDbService)
        {
            _configService = configService;
            _elementDbService = elementDbService;

            // 鐩戝惉锛氫粠閰嶇疆椤靛悓姝ュ厓绱犲悕鍗?
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<ActiveConfigsChangedMessage>(this, (r, m) => {

                    if (ActiveElements.Count > 0 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedElement))
                        SelectedElement = ActiveElements[0];
                    else
                        UpdateFilteredData();
                });
            });

            // 鐩戝惉锛氭帴鏀舵潵鑷祴閲忔ā鍧楁垨瀵煎叆鐨勫疄楠屽叏閲忔暟鎹寘
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SampleSequenceChangedMessage>(this, (r, m) => {
                _rawFullSequence = m.Value;
                UpdateFilteredData();
            });

            // 鐩戝惉锛氭祴鏍峰畬鎴愬悗寮哄害鏁版嵁鐨勬洿鏂伴€氱煡
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<MeasurementDataUpdatedMessage>(this, (r, m) => {
                _rawFullSequence = m.Value;
                UpdateFilteredData();
            });

        #region 5. 鏍稿績閫昏緫寮曟搸

        [ObservableProperty] private bool _showAlert;

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏁版嵁鍒嗗尯閫昏緫锛氬皢鍘熷鏁版嵁鏍规嵁[褰撳墠閫夊畾鍏冪礌]鎷嗗垎涓衡€滃乏寰呮祴鈥濅笌鈥滃彸鏍囧噯鈥?
        /// </summary>
