        /// <summary>
        /// 銆愬凡搴熷純/鏂紑銆戯細灏嗕富鐣岄潰杩借釜鐨勭墿鐞嗗嘲绾胯浆鎹负鏈〉闈㈢殑涓氬姟鍏冪礌锛屽苟缁存姢 UI銆?
        /// </summary>
        public void RefreshElementsFromTracker()
        {
            // 閫昏緫宸叉柇寮€锛屼笉鍐嶅熀浜庡宄扮孩绾垮鍒犲厓绱犺
            /*
            // 蹇呴』鍦?UI 绾跨▼鎵ц锛屽洜涓烘秹鍙?ObservableCollection 鐨勪慨鏀?
            Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
            {
                // 1. 鑾峰彇涓荤晫闈㈠綋鍓嶆墍鏈夌孩绾垮搴旂殑鈥滄爣鍑嗗悕绉扳€濆垪琛?(Pb, Cd, 鎴?宄癅xxx)
                var currentTrackedNamesFromMain = _peakTracker.TrackedPeaks
                    .Select(p => MatchElement(p.CurrentWavelength))
                    .Distinct() // 鍘婚噸
                    .ToList();

                // 2. 鍚屾涓嬫媺妗?(PickedElements)锛氱Щ闄ゅ凡鍒犻櫎鐨勫嘲锛屾坊鍔犳柊鍙戠幇鐨勫嘲

                if (MeasurementSequence != null)

                    foreach (var sample in MeasurementSequence)

                        var rowsToRemove = sample.ElementConcentrations
                            .Where(ec => !currentTrackedNamesFromMain.Contains(ec.ElementName))
                            .ToList();
                        foreach (var row in rowsToRemove) sample.ElementConcentrations.Remove(row);

                if (!PickedElements.Contains(SelectedElement))
                {
                    SelectedElement = PickedElements.FirstOrDefault() ?? string.Empty;
                }
                
                // 5. 鏇存柊鍙充笂瑙掔殑娴嬮噺缁勪俊鎭睍绀?

            // 绠€鍗曟洿鏂颁竴涓嬪彸涓婅鐨勬祴閲忕粍淇℃伅鍗冲彲
            Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
            {
                UpdateMeasurementGroupDisplay();
            });
        }
        
        /// <summary>
        /// 鏇存柊鍙充笂瑙掆€滄牱鍝佷俊鎭€濆崱鐗囦腑灞曠ず鐨勫厓绱犲垎缁勶紙鎸夌浉鍚岀殑绉垎鏃堕棿鍜屽钩鍧囨鏁板垎缁勶級
        /// </summary>
        private void UpdateMeasurementGroupDisplay()
        {
            if (CurrentSample == null || _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs == null || _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs.Count == 0)
            {
                CurrentMeasurementGroup = new MeasurementGroup();
                return;
            }

            // 鍋囪褰撳墠鍙彇閰嶇疆涓殑绗竴缁勪綔涓哄睍绀猴紙鐢变簬鐩墠涓氬姟鏄悓鏃堕噰鎵€鏈夊嘲锛屽鏋滄湁涓嶅悓閰嶇疆锛岀‖浠跺疄鐜板垎缁勮疆璇㈡祴閲忕殑閫昏緫灏嗗湪杩欓噷椹卞姩锛?
            var firstConfig = _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs.FirstOrDefault();
            if (firstConfig == null) return;

            int currentIntegrationTime = firstConfig.IntegrationTime;
            int currentAverageCount = firstConfig.AveragingCount;

            // 鎵惧嚭鎵€鏈夊拰绗竴缁勫叿鏈夌浉鍚屻€愮Н鍒嗘椂闂淬€戝拰銆愬钩鍧囨鏁般€戠殑鍏冪礌
            var groupedElements = _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs
                .Where(c => c.IntegrationTime == currentIntegrationTime && c.AveragingCount == currentAverageCount)
                .Select(c => $"{c.ElementName}({c.Wavelength})")
                .ToList();

            // 涓氬姟閫昏緫淇敼锛氬鏋滃綋鍓嶆槸鈥滅┖鐧芥憾娑测€濓紝骞朵笖锛堝彧鏈変竴涓厓绱?鎴?鎵€鏈夊厓绱犵殑绉垎鏃堕棿鍜屽钩鍧囨鏁伴兘鐩稿悓锛?
            // 閭ｄ箞涓嶉渶瑕佹樉绀哄厓绱犱俊鎭€傚鍏冪礌涓旈厤缃笉鍚屾椂鎵嶉渶瑕佹樉绀恒€?
            bool isBlankSample = CurrentSample.Type == SampleType.绌虹櫧;
            var distinctConfigGroupsCount = _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs
                .Select(c => new { c.IntegrationTime, c.AveragingCount })
                .Distinct()
                .Count();

            if (isBlankSample && distinctConfigGroupsCount <= 1)
            {
                // 涓嶉渶瑕佹樉绀哄厓绱犱俊鎭紝娓呯┖鍒楄〃
                groupedElements.Clear();
            }

            CurrentMeasurementGroup = new MeasurementGroup
            {
                IntegrationTime = currentIntegrationTime,
                AverageCount = currentAverageCount,
                Elements = new ObservableCollection<string>(groupedElements)
            };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏅鸿兘鍖归厤锛氬皢瀵诲嘲鎹曟崏鍒扮殑娉㈤暱涓庣敤鎴烽厤缃殑鍏冪礌杩涜瀵归綈 (卤3.0nm瀹瑰樊)
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="peakedWavelength">瀵诲嘲绠楁硶鎹曟崏鍒扮殑鐗╃悊娉㈤暱</param>
        /// <returns>鍖归厤鍒扮殑鍏冪礌鍚?(濡?"Pb") 鎴栧師濮嬫尝闀垮瓧绗︿覆 (濡?"宄癅283.31")</returns>
        public string MatchElement(double peakedWavelength)
        {
            double tolerance = 3.0; // 璁惧畾 3.0nm 鐨勮嚜鍔ㄨ瘑鍒宸寖鍥?

            // 瀵绘壘璺濈褰撳墠宄颁綅鏈€杩戜笖鍦ㄥ宸寖鍥村唴鐨勫凡閰嶇疆鍏冪礌
            var matched = _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs
                .Select(c => new { Config = c, Diff = Math.Abs(c.Wavelength - peakedWavelength) })
                .Where(x => x.Diff <= tolerance)
                .OrderBy(x => x.Diff) // 纭繚鍖归厤鍒扮墿鐞嗚窛绂绘渶鎺ヨ繎鐨勯偅涓厓绱?
                .FirstOrDefault();

            if (matched != null)
            {
                return $"{matched.Config.ElementName}({matched.Config.Wavelength})"; // 鍖归厤鎴愬姛锛岃繑鍥炴牸寮忓寲鍚嶇О
            }
            return $"宄癅{peakedWavelength:F2}"; // 鍖归厤澶辫触锛岃繑鍥炲師濮嬫尝闀挎牸寮?
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鑾峰彇褰撳墠涓嬫媺妗嗛€変腑椤瑰搴旂殑鐗╃悊娉㈤暱鍊?(鐢ㄤ簬鍥捐〃缂╂斁鍜屾暟鎹噰闆?
        /// </summary>
        public double GetTargetWavelength()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedElement)) return 0;

            // 鍦烘櫙 1锛氬鏋滈€変腑椤规槸宸查厤缃殑鍏冪礌鍚?(濡?"Pb(283.31)")
            var config = _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs.FirstOrDefault(x => $"{x.ElementName}({x.Wavelength})" == SelectedElement || x.ElementName == SelectedElement);
            if (config != null) return config.Wavelength;

            // 鍦烘櫙 2锛氬鏋滈€変腑椤规槸鏈瘑鍒殑娉㈤暱瀛楃涓?(濡?"宄癅283.31")
            if (SelectedElement.Contains("@"))
            {
                string wlStr = SelectedElement.Split('@')[1].Replace("nm", "").Trim();
                return double.TryParse(wlStr, out double wl) ? wl : 0;
            }
            return 0;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 渚?View 灞傚湪姣忓抚娓叉煋鏃惰皟鐢紝灏嗗綋鍓嶇孩绾夸綅缃殑寮哄害鏁版嵁鎺ㄥ叆澶氶€氶亾缂撳啿鍖?
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="name">鍏冪礌鍚嶆垨宄颁綅瀛楃涓?/param>
        /// <param name="intensity">褰撳墠甯х殑寮哄害鍊?/param>
        public void PushData(string name, double intensity)
        {
            if (!IsCollecting) return; // 鍙湁鍦ㄩ噰闆嗙姸鎬佹墠绉疮鏁版嵁

            // 纭繚瀛楀吀涓湁璇ラ€氶亾鐨勫垪琛?
            if (!_multiChannelBuffer.ContainsKey(name))
                _multiChannelBuffer[name] = new List<double>();

            _multiChannelBuffer[name].Add(intensity); // 灏嗘暟鎹偣鍔犲叆瀵瑰簲閫氶亾鐨勭紦鍐插尯
        }

        #endregion

        #region 浜や簰鍛戒护

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task StartCollecting()
        {
            if (CurrentSample == null) { MessageBox.Show("璇峰厛閫夋嫨宸︿晶鐨勬牱鍝侊紒", "璀﹀憡"); return; }
            if (PickedElements.Count == 0) { MessageBox.Show("璇峰厛鍦ㄤ富鐣岄潰瀵诲嘲閫夋嫨瑕佺洃鎺х殑鍏冪礌锛?, "璀﹀憡"); return; }

            // 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚︽湁鍦ㄧ嚎鐨勫厜璋变华璁惧
            if (SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices.Count == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("鏈娴嬪埌鍦ㄧ嚎鐨勫厜璋变华锛岃妫€鏌ヨ澶囪繛鎺ュ悗鍐嶈瘯锛?, "纭欢鏈氨缁?, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            // 妫€鏌ュ凡杩炴帴鐨勫厜璋变华鏄惁寮€鍚簡鈥滆繛缁噰闆嗏€濇祦
            if (SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices.Any(d => !d.IsMeasuring))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("鍏夎氨浠凡杩炴帴锛屼絾灏氭湭鍚姩杩炵画閲囬泦娴侊紒\n璇风‘淇濆厜璋变华宸插浜庡伐浣?璇诲彇鐘舵€佸悗鍐嶅紑濮嬪叏鑷姩娴嬮噺銆?, "閲囬泦鏈惎鍔?, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            // 銆愬皬鏍峰搧妯″紡鐗规湁銆戞鏌ラ噰闆嗛厤缃槸鍚﹁秴鏃?(涓婇檺 1 鍒嗛挓)
            if (IsSmallSampleMode)
            {
                long totalEstimatedTimeMs = 0;
                var groups = _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs.GroupBy(c => new { c.IntegrationTime, c.AveragingCount }).ToList();
                foreach (var group in groups)
                {
                    long intTime = group.Key.IntegrationTime;
                    long avgCount = group.Key.AveragingCount;
                    long hardwareDelay = 3 * intTime * avgCount;
                    
                    long measurementTime = CurrentSample.Repeats * (intTime * avgCount);
                    long intervalDelay = (CurrentSample.Repeats - 1) * (long)(CurrentSample.Interval * 1000);
                    if (intervalDelay < 0) intervalDelay = 0;
                
                    totalEstimatedTimeMs += hardwareDelay + measurementTime + intervalDelay;
                }

                if (totalEstimatedTimeMs > 60000)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("褰撳墠閰嶇疆鐨勯噰鏍锋€绘椂闀胯秴杩囦簡 1 鍒嗛挓闄愬埗锛乗n鍦ㄥ皬鏍峰搧妯″紡涓嬶紝浠庡宄扮粨鏉熷埌鏈夋晥淇″彿閲囬泦瀹屾瘯涓嶈兘瓒呰繃 1 鍒嗛挓锛岃鍑忓皯閲囨牱娆℃暟銆佺缉鐭棿闅旀椂闂存垨闄嶄綆绉垎骞冲潎鍙傛暟銆?, "閰嶇疆閿欒", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }
            }

            // 鍚姩纭寮圭獥锛堟樉绀烘牱鍝佷俊鎭€佸厓绱犳尝闀垮強娴撳害锛?
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            sb.AppendLine($"褰撳墠娴嬫牱搴忓垪锛歿CurrentSample.SampleName}");
            if (CurrentSample.Type == GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.Messages.SampleType.鏍囨恫)
            {
                sb.AppendLine("绫诲瀷锛氭爣娑?);
                sb.AppendLine("鍏冪礌閰嶇疆鍙婃祿搴︼細");
                foreach (var ec in CurrentSample.ElementConcentrations)
                {
                    sb.AppendLine($" - {ec.ElementName}: {ec.ConcentrationValue} {CurrentSample.ConcentrationUnit}");
                }
            }
            else
            {
                sb.AppendLine("绫诲瀷锛氬緟娴嬫恫");
                sb.AppendLine("鍏冪礌閰嶇疆锛?);
                foreach (var ec in CurrentSample.ElementConcentrations)
                {
                    sb.AppendLine($" - {ec.ElementName}");
                }
            }
            sb.AppendLine("\n璇风‘璁ゆ斁缃ソ褰撳墠鏍峰搧鍚庯紝鐐瑰嚮鈥滅‘瀹氣€濆紑濮嬫祴閲忋€?);

            var result = MessageBox.Show(sb.ToString(), "寮€濮嬮噰闆嗙‘璁?, MessageBoxButton.OKCancel, MessageBoxImage.Information);
            if (result != MessageBoxResult.OK) return;

            IsCollecting = true;
            await RunAutomatedMeasurementAsync();
        }

        private async Task RunAutomatedMeasurementAsync()
        {
            while (CurrentSample != null && IsCollecting)
            {
                // 濡傛灉璇ュ簭鍒楀凡缁忔祴閲忚繃锛屽垯娓呯┖涔嬪墠鐨勬暟鎹?
                if (CurrentSample.Status == "宸插畬鎴?)
                {
                    foreach (var ec in CurrentSample.ElementConcentrations)
                    {
                        ec.MeasuredIntensity = 0;
                        ec.MeasuredRsd = 0;
                        foreach (var rep in ec.Reps)
                        {
                            rep.Intensity = null;
                        }
                    }
                    // 鍙戦€佹秷鎭€氱煡鐣岄潰鏁版嵁宸茶娓呯┖
                    CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Messaging.WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(
                        new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.Messages.MeasurementDataUpdatedMessage(MeasurementSequence.ToList())
                    );
                }

                CurrentSample.Status = "閲囬泦鏁版嵁涓?..";

                // 銆愬皬鏍峰搧妯″紡鐗规湁銆戝墠缃‖浠惰皟搴﹀姩浣?
                if (IsSmallSampleMode)
                {
                    CurrentSample.Status = "杩涘畾閲忕幆...";

                    // Step 1: 杞悜闃€闂ㄥ垏鑷抽€氶亾 2 (杩炵画)锛岀瓑寰?1 绉?
                    _generalService.ControlSteeringValve(false); 
                    await Task.Delay(1000);
                
                    // Step 2: 娉ㄥ皠娉垫寜鏈€澶ц绋?3000)鎶芥帹 5 娆?
                    // 鐙珛浜庝富鐣岄潰鍗＄墖閰嶇疆锛氬己鍒朵娇鐢?姝ｅ悜 (true)
                    bool initialPort = true;
                    int motionTime = 4500; // 琛岀▼4500ms
                    int restTime = 1000;   // 鍋滈】1000ms
                
                    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
                    {
                        if (!IsCollecting) break;

                        // 鎶戒竴涓?(3000琛岀▼)
                        _generalService.ControlSyringePump(initialPort, 3000);
                        await Task.Delay(motionTime + restTime); // 4.5s杩愯 + 1s鍋滈】
                        if (!IsCollecting) break;
                
                        // 鎺ㄤ竴涓?(褰掗浂)
                        _generalService.ControlSyringePump(!initialPort, 0);
                        await Task.Delay(motionTime + restTime); // 4.5s杩愯 + 1s鍋滈】
                    }
                    
                    if (!IsCollecting) break; // 涓€斿彇娑?

                    // Step 3: 杞悜闃€闂ㄥ垏鑷抽€氶亾 1 (瀹氶噺鐜?
                    _generalService.ControlSteeringValve(true);
                
                    // Step 4: 绛夊緟 1 鍒嗛挓 (60000ms) 鍝嶅簲鍙栨秷鍒嗘绛夊緟
                    CurrentSample.Status = "杩涙縺鍙戝尯...";
                    for (int w = 0; w < 60; w++)
                    {
                        if (!IsCollecting) break;
                        await Task.Delay(1000);
                    }
                    if (!IsCollecting) break;
                    
                    CurrentSample.Status = "閲囬泦鏁版嵁涓?..";
                }

                // 鎸夌Н鍒嗘椂闂村拰骞冲潎娆℃暟鍒嗙粍
                var groups = _elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs
                    .GroupBy(c => new { c.IntegrationTime, c.AveragingCount })
                    .ToList();

                var cachedColumns = new List<(string, SpectralData)>();

                foreach (var group in groups)
                {
                    if (!IsCollecting) break;

                    int intTime = group.Key.IntegrationTime;
                    int avgCount = group.Key.AveragingCount;

                    string elementsHeader = string.Join(" | ", group.Select(c => $"{c.ElementName}({c.Wavelength})"));
                    string groupHeader = $"{elementsHeader} - {intTime}ms x{avgCount}";

                    // 涓嬪彂纭欢閰嶇疆
                    foreach(var device in SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices)
                    {
                        await device.UpdateConfigurationAsync(intTime, (uint)avgCount);
                        CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Messaging.WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.Messages.HardwareConfigChangedMessage(device.Config.SerialNumber, intTime, (uint)avgCount));
                    }

                    // 鍒囨崲鍙傛暟鍚庣瓑寰呯‖浠剁ǔ瀹? 3 * (绉垎鏃堕棿 * 骞冲潎娆℃暟)
                    await Task.Delay(3 * intTime * avgCount);

                    for (int r = 0; r < CurrentSample.Repeats; r++)
                    {
                        if (!IsCollecting) break;

                        // UI 鏇存柊娴嬮噺涓姸鎬?
                        var repModels = new List<MeasurementRepModel>();
                        foreach (var conf in group)
                        {
                            string matchName = $"{conf.ElementName}({conf.Wavelength})";
                            var targetRow = CurrentSample.ElementConcentrations.FirstOrDefault(e => e.ElementName == matchName || e.ElementName == conf.ElementName);
                            if (targetRow != null && r < targetRow.Reps.Count)
                            {
                                targetRow.Reps[r].IsMeasuring = true;
                                repModels.Add(targetRow.Reps[r]);
                            }
                        }

                        // 浠庣‖浠惰繛缁暟鎹祦涓埅鍙栨渶鏂颁竴甯?
                        SpectralData frame = await WaitForNextFrameAsync();

                        // 鎭㈠鐘舵€?
                        foreach (var rep in repModels) rep.IsMeasuring = false;

                        if (frame != null)
                        {
                            cachedColumns.Add((groupHeader + $" [绗瑊r + 1}娆", frame));

                            // 鎻愬彇娉㈤暱寮哄害
                            foreach (var conf in group)
                            {
                                double realWl = SpectrometerLogic.GetActualPeakWavelength(frame, conf.Wavelength, 1.0);
                                double realIntensity = SpectrometerLogic.GetIntensityAtWavelength(frame, realWl);

                                string matchName = $"{conf.ElementName}({conf.Wavelength})";
                                var targetRow = CurrentSample.ElementConcentrations.FirstOrDefault(e => e.ElementName == matchName || e.ElementName == conf.ElementName);
                                if (targetRow != null && r < targetRow.Reps.Count)
                                {
                                    targetRow.Reps[r].Intensity = Math.Round(realIntensity, 0);
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // 闂撮殧绛夊緟 (闈炴渶鍚庝竴娆?
                        if (r < CurrentSample.Repeats - 1)
                        {
                            int delayMs = (int)(CurrentSample.Interval * 1000);
                            if (delayMs > 0) await Task.Delay(delayMs);
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (!IsCollecting) break; // 涓€斿彇娑?

                // 璁＄畻 RSD 鍜?骞冲潎鍊?
                foreach (var row in CurrentSample.ElementConcentrations)
                {
                    var validIntensities = row.Reps.Where(r => r.Intensity.HasValue).Select(r => r.Intensity.Value).ToList();
                    if (validIntensities.Count >= 2)
                    {
                        double avg = validIntensities.Average();
                        double sumOfSquares = validIntensities.Select(val => (val - avg) * (val - avg)).Sum();
                        double stdDev = Math.Sqrt(sumOfSquares / (validIntensities.Count - 1));
                        double rsd = (avg != 0) ? (stdDev / avg) * 100.0 : 0;

                        row.MeasuredIntensity = Math.Round(avg, 0);
                        row.MeasuredRsd = Math.Round(rsd, 2);
                    }
                    else if (validIntensities.Count == 1)
                    {
                        row.MeasuredIntensity = Math.Round(validIntensities[0], 0);
                        row.MeasuredRsd = 0;
                    }
                }

                CurrentSample.Status = "宸插畬鎴?;

                // 鍚庡彴 CSV 钀界洏
                string folder = System.IO.Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "Records");
                string filePath = System.IO.Path.Combine(folder, $"{CurrentSample.SampleName}_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}.csv");
                CurrentSample.CsvFilePath = filePath;
                
                _ = Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        await CsvExportService.ExportSpectralDataColumnsAsync(filePath, cachedColumns);
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => MessageBox.Show($"CSV瀵煎嚭澶辫触: {ex.Message}"));
                    }
                });

                // 鏈湴 JSON 搴忓垪淇濆瓨
                _configService.SaveResults(MeasurementSequence.ToList());

                // 閫氱煡鍏ㄧ郴缁熸暟鎹凡鏇存柊锛堣繖浼氳鈥滄暟鎹鐞嗏€濋〉闈㈠埛鏂版嬁鍒板己搴︽暟鎹級
                CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Messaging.WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(
                    new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Models.Messages.MeasurementDataUpdatedMessage(MeasurementSequence.ToList())
                );

                // 銆愪慨鏀广€戯細鍦ㄥ脊绐楅樆濉炵嚎绋嬩箣鍓嶏紝鎻愬墠灏?IsCollecting 缃负 false锛?
                // 浣垮緱缁戝畾璇ュ睘鎬х殑鎸夐挳绔嬪埢鐢扁€滅粓姝㈤噰闆嗏€濆彉鍥炩€滃紑濮嬮噰闆嗏€濇牱寮忋€?
                IsCollecting = false;

                // 鑷姩璺冲悜涓嬩竴涓牱鍝侊紝骞剁粓姝㈡湰杞嚜鍔ㄥ惊鐜噰闆?
                int currentIndex = MeasurementSequence.IndexOf(CurrentSample);
                if (currentIndex < MeasurementSequence.Count - 1)
                {
                    var nextSample = MeasurementSequence[currentIndex + 1];
                    MessageBox.Show($"鏍峰搧 [{CurrentSample.SampleName}] 娴嬮噺瀹屾瘯锛乗n\n璇峰噯澶囦笅涓€涓牱鍝?[{nextSample.SampleName}]銆?, "鎻愮ず", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                    CurrentSample = nextSample;
                }
                else
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("鏁翠釜娴嬮噺搴忓垪宸插叏閮ㄥ畬鎴愶紒\n鎵€鏈夊簭鍒楅兘閲囬泦瀹屾垚鍚庯紝璇风Щ姝ユ暟鎹鐞嗭紒", "鎻愮ず", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                }
                
                break;
            }
        }

        private Task<SpectralData> WaitForNextFrameAsync()
        {
            var tcs = new TaskCompletionSource<SpectralData>();
            var token = new object();

            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SpectralDataMessage>(token, (r, m) =>
            {
                WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Unregister<SpectralDataMessage>(token);
                tcs.TrySetResult(m.Value);
            });

            // 15绉掕秴鏃朵繚鎶?
            Task.Delay(15000).ContinueWith(_ => 
            {
                WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Unregister<SpectralDataMessage>(token);
                tcs.TrySetResult(null); 
            });

            return tcs.Task;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 绱ф€ュ仠姝㈠懡浠わ細寮哄埗鍋滄閲囬泦銆?
        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand] private void StopSequence() => IsCollecting = false;

        /// <summary>
        /// 瀵煎嚭鏈浼氳瘽娴嬮噺鏁版嵁涓?JSON 鏂囦欢銆?
        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void ExportCurrentSession()
        {
            if (MeasurementSequence.Count == 0) return;

            var dialog = new Microsoft.Win32.SaveFileDialog
            {
                Filter = "瀹為獙蹇収鏂囦欢 (*.json)|*.json",
                FileName = $"瀹為獙鏁版嵁_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmm}",
                Title = "瀵煎嚭褰撳墠娴嬮噺搴忓垪鍙婄粨鏋?
            };

            if (dialog.ShowDialog() == true)
            {
                try
                {
                    _configService.ExportResults(dialog.FileName, MeasurementSequence.ToList());
                    MessageBox.Show("褰撳墠浼氳瘽鏁版嵁瀵煎嚭鎴愬姛锛?, "鎻愮ず");
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show($"瀵煎嚭澶辫触: {ex.Message}", "閿欒");
                }
            }
        }

        #endregion
    }
}

        partial void OnSelectedTemplateChanged(string value)
        {
            if (_isHandlingTemplateChange) return;

            if (Samples.Count > 0)

                if (result != MessageBoxResult.Yes)
                {
                    Application.Current.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(() =>
                    {
                        _isHandlingTemplateChange = true;
                        SelectedTemplate = string.IsNullOrEmpty(_previousTemplate) ? "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉? : _previousTemplate;
                        _isHandlingTemplateChange = false;
                    }), System.Windows.Threading.DispatcherPriority.Background);
                    return;
                }
            }

            LoadTemplate();
        }

        [ObservableProperty]
        private string _newTemplateName = string.Empty;

            config.LastSampleRepeats = value;
            _configService.Save(config);

        partial void OnIsRestrictedToUnknownOnlyChanged(bool value)
        {
            AvailableSampleTypes.Clear();
            if (value)
            {
                AvailableSampleTypes.Add(SampleType.寰呮祴娑?;
            }
            else
            {
                foreach (SampleType type in Enum.GetValues(typeof(SampleType)))

            if (value)
            {
                foreach (var sample in Samples)
                {
                    if (sample.Type != SampleType.寰呮祴娑?
                        sample.Type = SampleType.寰呮祴娑?
                }
            }
        }
        public List<int> AvailableRepeats { get; } = Enumerable.Range(1, 99).ToList();

        public SampleSequenceViewModel(ElementConfigViewModel elementConfigVM, JsonConfigService configService)
        {
            _elementConfigVM = elementConfigVM;
            _configService = configService;

            var config = _configService.Load();
            _batchStandardCount = config.LastBatchStandardCount;
            _batchUnknownCount = config.LastBatchUnknownCount;
            _globalRepeats = config.LastSampleRepeats;
            _globalInterval = config.LastGlobalInterval;
            _concentrationUnit = string.IsNullOrEmpty(config.LastConcentrationUnit) ? "ppm" : config.LastConcentrationUnit;

            // 鍒犻櫎鏂囦欢鍚庡埛鏂板垪琛紝姝ゆ椂涓嬫媺妗嗗皢鍙樹负绌虹櫧
            RefreshTemplates();

            // 鍒濆鍖栨椂纭繚鏍峰搧鍒楄〃涓虹┖锛屽苟鐩戝惉闆嗗悎鍙樺寲瀹炵幇閲嶅悕瀹炴椂鏍￠獙
            Samples.Clear();
            Samples.CollectionChanged += Samples_CollectionChanged;

            // 鎷夊彇褰撳墠宸查€夌殑鍒嗘瀽鍏冪礌
            UpdateActiveElements(_elementConfigVM.SelectedConfigs.ToList());

            // 娉ㄥ唽娑堟伅鐩戝惉
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<ActiveConfigsChangedMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => UpdateActiveElements(m.Value));
            });

            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SampleTypeChangedMessage>(this, (r, m) => RenameSampleSmartly(m.Value));

            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SampleSequenceViewModel, SequenceStatusRequestMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                m.Reply(r.IsSequenceApplied || r.Samples.Count > 0);
            });

            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SampleSequenceViewModel, ClearSequenceRequestMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                {
                    r.IsSequenceApplied = false;
                    r.Samples.Clear();
                    // 鍚戜笅娓革紙娴嬮噺鐣岄潰鍜屾暟鎹鐞嗙晫闈級鍙戦€佷竴涓┖搴忓垪锛屽苟甯︽湁寮哄埗瑕嗙洊鏍囪锛屽交搴曟竻绌洪仐鐣欐暟鎹?
                    WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SampleSequenceChangedMessage(new List<SampleItemModel>(), true));
                });
            });
        }

        // --- 鏍稿績閫昏緫鏂规硶 ---

        private void Samples_CollectionChanged(object? sender, System.Collections.Specialized.NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            IsSequenceApplied = false;

            if (e.OldItems != null)
            {
                foreach (SampleItemModel item in e.OldItems)
                    item.PropertyChanged -= OnSamplePropertyChanged;
            }
            if (e.NewItems != null)
            {
                foreach (SampleItemModel item in e.NewItems)
                    item.PropertyChanged += OnSamplePropertyChanged;
            }
        }

        private void OnSamplePropertyChanged(object? sender, System.ComponentModel.PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            IsSequenceApplied = false;

            if (e.PropertyName == nameof(SampleItemModel.SampleName))
            {
                var changedSample = sender as SampleItemModel;
                if (changedSample == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(changedSample.SampleName)) return;

                var count = Samples.Count(s => s.SampleName == changedSample.SampleName);
                if (count > 1)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show($"鏍峰搧鍚嶇О '{changedSample.SampleName}' 宸插瓨鍦紒绯荤粺宸茶嚜鍔ㄦ坊鍔犲悗缂€浠ュ尯鍒嗐€?, "鍚嶇О閲嶅", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    
                    string baseName = changedSample.SampleName;
                    int index = 1;
                    while (Samples.Any(s => s != changedSample && s.SampleName == $"{baseName}_{index}"))
                    {
                        index++;
                    }
                    changedSample.SampleName = $"{baseName}_{index}";
                }
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void DecreaseRepeats()
        {
            if (GlobalRepeats > 1) GlobalRepeats--;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void IncreaseRepeats()
        {
            if (GlobalRepeats < 99) GlobalRepeats++;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏇存柊褰撳墠鐨勬椿璺冨厓绱犲悕鍗曪紝琛ラ綈鎵€鏈夋牱鍝佺殑娴撳害妲戒綅锛屽苟閫氱煡 View 閲嶇粯鍒?
        /// </summary>
        private void UpdateActiveElements(List<AnalysisConfigItem> configs)
        {
            if (configs.Count > 0)
            {
                // 鍙湁褰撱€愭墍鏈夈€戝厓绱犻兘浣跨敤浜嗗巻鍙叉洸绾挎椂锛屾墠闄愬埗鍙兘娴嬧€滃緟娴嬫恫鈥?
                IsRestrictedToUnknownOnly = configs.All(c => c.FittingCurve != "娴嬮噺鏍″噯鏇茬嚎");
            }
            else
            {
                IsRestrictedToUnknownOnly = false;
            }

            _activeElements = configs.Select(x => 
                x.ElementName.Contains("(") ? x.ElementName : $"{x.ElementName}({x.Wavelength})"
            ).Distinct().ToList();

            // 涓ユ牸瀵归綈鐜版湁鏍峰搧鐨勫厓绱犳Ы浣嶅拰椤哄簭锛岄槻姝㈠姩鎬佸垪鏁版嵁閿欎綅
            foreach (var sample in Samples)
            {
                var newConcentrations = new System.Collections.ObjectModel.ObservableCollection<ElementConcentrationModel>();
                foreach (var elName in _activeElements)
                {
                    var existing = sample.ElementConcentrations.FirstOrDefault(c => c.ElementName == elName);
                    if (existing != null)
                    {
                        newConcentrations.Add(existing);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        newConcentrations.Add(new ElementConcentrationModel { ElementName = elName, ConcentrationValue = "" });
                    }
                }
                sample.ElementConcentrations = newConcentrations;
            }

            // 鍙戦€佹秷鎭 View (SampleSequenceView.xaml.cs) 鎵ц鍔ㄦ€佸垪鏋勫缓
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new RebuildColumnsMessage(_activeElements));
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        public void RefreshTemplates()
        {
            _isHandlingTemplateChange = true;
            
            var oldSelected = SelectedTemplate;
            SavedTemplates.Clear();
            
            // 鍙湁鍦ㄤ竴寮€濮嬫病閫夋嫨妯℃澘锛屾垨鑰呰閲嶇疆鏃讹紝鎵嶅湪鍒楄〃涓姞鍏モ€滄湭浣跨敤妯℃澘鈥?
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(oldSelected) || oldSelected == "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?)
            {
                SavedTemplates.Add("鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?);
            }
            
            foreach (var t in _storageService.GetSavedTemplates()) SavedTemplates.Add(t);
            
            if (SavedTemplates.Contains(oldSelected))
                SelectedTemplate = oldSelected;
            else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(oldSelected) && oldSelected != "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?)
            {
                // 濡傛灉涔嬪墠閫夋嫨鐨勬ā鏉胯鍒犻櫎浜嗭紝鍥為€€鍒版湭浣跨敤妯℃澘
                SavedTemplates.Insert(0, "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?);
                SelectedTemplate = "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?;
            }
            else
                SelectedTemplate = "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?;
                
            _isHandlingTemplateChange = false;
        }

        // --- 椤舵爮鍛戒护瀹炵幇 ---

        [RelayCommand]
        private void LoadTemplate()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedTemplate) || SelectedTemplate == "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?) return;

            var data = _storageService.LoadTemplate(SelectedTemplate);
            if (data != null)
            {
                _isDirtyOverwrite = true;
                Samples.Clear();
                // 璇嗗埆妯℃澘涓殑鍏冪礌鍚嶅崟锛屾彁鍙栧悕绉板拰娉㈤暱
                var templateElements = data.FirstOrDefault()?.ElementConcentrations
                                           .Select(c =>
                                           {
                                               string raw = c.ElementName;
                                               string symbol = raw;
                                               double wl = 0;
                                               int idx = raw.IndexOf('(');
                                               if (idx > 0)
                                               {
                                                   symbol = raw.Substring(0, idx);
                                                   string wlStr = raw.Substring(idx + 1).TrimEnd(')');
                                                   double.TryParse(wlStr, out wl);
                                               }
                                               return new AnalysisConfigItem { ElementName = symbol, Wavelength = wl };
                                           })
                                           .ToList() ?? new List<AnalysisConfigItem>();

                foreach (var item in data) Samples.Add(item);

                // 浠庢ā鏉夸腑鎭㈠椤堕儴鐨勨€滄爣鍑嗘牱鍝佲€濄€佲€滃緟娴嬫牱鍝佲€濄€佲€滈噸澶嶆鏁扳€濄€佲€滈棿闅斺€濆拰鈥滄祿搴﹀崟浣嶁€濋厤缃?
                if (data.Count > 0)
                {
                    GlobalRepeats = data[0].Repeats > 0 ? data[0].Repeats : 1;
                    GlobalInterval = data[0].Interval >= 0 ? data[0].Interval : 0.0;
                    
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(data[0].ConcentrationUnit))
                    {
                        ConcentrationUnit = data[0].ConcentrationUnit;
                    }

                    // 缁熻妯℃澘涓殑鏍囨恫鍜屽緟娴嬫恫鏁伴噺锛屽弽鍚戞洿鏂扮粰涓婃柟鐢熸垚鍣?
                    BatchStandardCount = data.Count(s => s.Type == SampleType.鏍囨恫);
                    BatchUnknownCount = data.Count(s => s.Type == SampleType.寰呮祴娑?;
                }

                // 閫氱煡鍏冪礌閰嶇疆椤甸潰鍚屾鏇存柊搴曞眰鏁版嵁
                WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SyncTemplateElementsMessage(templateElements));
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenTemplateFolder()
        {
            string path = _storageService.GetFolderPath();
            if (System.IO.Directory.Exists(path))
            {
                System.Diagnostics.Process.Start("explorer.exe", path);
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void SaveTemplate()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NewTemplateName))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("璇疯緭鍏ユā鏉垮悕绉?, "鎻愮ず", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            var existing = _storageService.GetSavedTemplates();
            if (existing.Contains(NewTemplateName))
            {
                var result = MessageBox.Show($"妯℃澘 '{NewTemplateName}' 宸插瓨鍦紝鏄惁瑕嗙洊锛?, "閲嶅悕纭", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                if (result == MessageBoxResult.No) return;
            }

            // 淇濆瓨鍓嶏紝灏嗛《鏍忕殑鍏ㄥ眬鍙傛暟寮哄埗鍐欏叆姣忎竴琛屾暟鎹腑锛岀‘淇濊兘琚簭鍒楀寲淇濆瓨
            foreach (var s in Samples)
            {
                s.Repeats = GlobalRepeats;
                s.Interval = GlobalInterval;
                s.ConcentrationUnit = ConcentrationUnit;
            }

            _storageService.SaveTemplate(NewTemplateName, Samples.ToList());
            RefreshTemplates();
            MessageBox.Show("搴忓垪妯℃澘淇濆瓨鎴愬姛锛?, "鎴愬姛", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void ImportCsv()
        {
            var dialog = new OpenFileDialog { Filter = "CSV 鏂囦欢 (*.csv)|*.csv" };
            if (dialog.ShowDialog() == true)
            {
                try
                {
                    // 浣跨敤澧炲己鐗?Service 瀵煎叆
                    var importedData = _storageService.ImportFromCsv(dialog.FileName, out var detectedElements);

                    var newConfigs = detectedElements.Select(e => new AnalysisConfigItem { ElementName = e }).ToList();

                    _isDirtyOverwrite = true;
                    Samples.Clear();
                    foreach (var item in importedData) Samples.Add(item);

                    // 瑙﹀彂鐣岄潰鏇存柊
                    UpdateActiveElements(newConfigs);
                    MessageBox.Show("CSV 瀵煎叆鎴愬姛锛?, "鎴愬姛", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show($"瀵煎叆澶辫触: {ex.Message}", "閿欒", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
                }
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void ExportCsv()
        {
            if (Samples.Count == 0) return;

            var dialog = new SaveFileDialog
            {
                Filter = "CSV 鏂囦欢 (*.csv)|*.csv",
                FileName = $"搴忓垪瀵煎嚭_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmm}"
            };

            if (dialog.ShowDialog() == true)
            {
                // 瀵煎嚭褰撳墠鏄剧ず鐨勬牱鍝佸強鎵€鏈夊姩鎬佹祿搴﹀垪
                _storageService.ExportToCsv(dialog.FileName, Samples.ToList(), _activeElements);
                MessageBox.Show("瀵煎嚭 CSV 鎴愬姛锛?, "鎴愬姛", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
            }
        }

        private bool _isDirtyOverwrite = true;

        // --- 琛ㄦ牸鎿嶄綔鍛戒护 ---

        [RelayCommand]
        private void BatchGenerate()
        {
            if (Samples.Count > 0)
            {
                var result = MessageBox.Show("涓€閿敓鎴愬皢娓呯┖褰撳墠鎵€鏈夋暟鎹紝纭缁х画鍚楋紵", "璀﹀憡", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                if (result == MessageBoxResult.No) return;
            }

            _isDirtyOverwrite = true;
            _isHandlingTemplateChange = true;
            if (!SavedTemplates.Contains("鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?))
                SavedTemplates.Insert(0, "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?);
            SelectedTemplate = "鏈娇鐢ㄦā鏉?;
            _isHandlingTemplateChange = false;
            Samples.Clear();

            // 鐢熸垚鏂板簭鍒楁椂锛屼繚鐣欏綋鍓嶅凡閫夌殑鍏冪礌閰嶇疆锛屼笉鍐嶆竻绌?
            // WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SyncTemplateElementsMessage(new List<AnalysisConfigItem>()));

            if (IsRestrictedToUnknownOnly)
            {
                int unknownCount = BatchUnknownCount; // 鍏佽涓?
                for (int i = 1; i <= unknownCount; i++)
                    Samples.Add(CreateNewSample(SampleType.寰呮祴娑? $"寰呮祴娑?{i}"));
                
                MessageBox.Show("鍥犲綋鍓嶅厓绱犻厤缃娇鐢ㄤ簡宸蹭繚瀛樼殑鎷熷悎鏇茬嚎锛屽凡鑷姩涓烘偍鐪佺暐绌虹櫧涓庢爣娑诧紝浠呯敓鎴愬緟娴嬫恫銆?, "鎻愮ず", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
            }
            else
            {
                if (BatchStandardCount == 0 && BatchUnknownCount != 0)
                {
                    // 鐢熸垚寰呮祴鏍峰簭鍒楋紝鏃犵┖鐧?
                    for (int i = 1; i <= BatchUnknownCount; i++)
                        Samples.Add(CreateNewSample(SampleType.寰呮祴娑? $"寰呮祴娑?{i}"));
                }
                else
                {
                    // 鐢熸垚绌虹櫧
                    Samples.Add(CreateNewSample(SampleType.绌虹櫧, "BLK-1"));
                    // 鐢熸垚鏍囧噯鍝佸簭鍒?
                    for (int i = 1; i <= BatchStandardCount; i++)
                        Samples.Add(CreateNewSample(SampleType.鏍囨恫, $"STD-{i}"));
                    // 鐢熸垚寰呮祴鏍峰簭鍒?
                    for (int i = 1; i <= BatchUnknownCount; i++)
                        Samples.Add(CreateNewSample(SampleType.寰呮祴娑? $"寰呮祴娑?{i}"));
                }
            }

            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new RebuildColumnsMessage(_activeElements));
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void AddRow() => Samples.Add(CreateNewSample(SampleType.寰呮祴娑? "鏂版牱鍝?));

        [RelayCommand]
        private void DeleteRow()
        {
            if (SelectedSample != null)
            {
                var result = MessageBox.Show($"纭畾瑕佸垹闄ら€変腑琛?({SelectedSample.SampleName}) 鍚楋紵", "纭鍒犻櫎", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Question);
                if (result == MessageBoxResult.Yes)
                {
                    Samples.Remove(SelectedSample);
                    SelectedSample = null;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                MessageBox.Show("璇峰厛鍦ㄨ〃鏍间腑閫変腑瑕佸垹闄ょ殑琛岋紒", "鎻愮ず", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void ApplySequence()
        {
            if (Samples == null || Samples.Count == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("褰撳墠鏍峰搧搴忓垪涓虹┖锛岃鍏堟坊鍔犳牱鍝佹垨鐢熸垚搴忓垪鍚庡啀搴旂敤锛?, "璀﹀憡", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (_activeElements == null || _activeElements.Count == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("褰撳墠鏈€夋嫨浠讳綍鍒嗘瀽鍏冪礌锛岃鍏堝湪鈥滃厓绱犻厤缃€濈晫闈㈠姞鍏ュ厓绱狅紒", "璀﹀憡", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (IsRestrictedToUnknownOnly && Samples.Any(s => s.Type != SampleType.寰呮祴娑?)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("搴旂敤澶辫触锛氬綋鍓嶅厓绱犻厤缃娇鐢ㄤ簡宸蹭繚瀛樼殑鎷熷悎鏇茬嚎锛屼笉鍏佽娴嬮噺鏍囨恫鎴栫┖鐧斤紒\n\n璇蜂慨鏀圭浉鍏虫牱鍝佺被鍨嬶紝鎴栬繑鍥炩€滃厓绱犻厤缃€濇洿鏀规洸绾胯缃€?, "搴旂敤琚嫤鎴?, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
                return;
            }

            // 妫€鏌ユ爣娑茬殑娴撳害鏄惁閮藉凡濉啓
            var incompleteStandards = Samples.Where(s => s.Type == SampleType.鏍囨恫 && 
                                                         s.ElementConcentrations.Any(c => string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(c.ConcentrationValue)))
                                             .Select(s => s.SampleName).ToList();
            if (incompleteStandards.Count > 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"浠ヤ笅鏍囨恫鐨勫厓绱犳祿搴︽湭瀹屽叏濉啓锛歕n{string.Join(", ", incompleteStandards)}\n\n璇峰皢鏍囨恫鐨勬墍鏈夋祿搴﹁ˉ鍏呭畬鏁村悗鍐嶅簲鐢紒", "鏍囨恫娴撳害缂哄け鎷︽埅", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            // 妫€鏌ュ悓涓€鍏冪礌鐨勬爣娑叉祿搴︽槸鍚﹂噸澶?
            var standardSamples = Samples.Where(s => s.Type == SampleType.鏍囨恫).ToList();
            if (standardSamples.Count > 1)
            {
                var elementNames = standardSamples.First().ElementConcentrations.Select(c => c.ElementName).ToList();
                foreach (var elementName in elementNames)
                {
                    var concentrations = new HashSet<double>();
                    foreach (var sample in standardSamples)
                    {
                        var concModel = sample.ElementConcentrations.FirstOrDefault(c => c.ElementName == elementName);
                        if (concModel != null && double.TryParse(concModel.ConcentrationValue, out double concValue))
                        {
                            if (!concentrations.Add(concValue))
                            {
                                MessageBox.Show($"搴旂敤澶辫触锛氬厓绱?'{elementName}' 鍦ㄥ涓爣鍑嗘憾娑蹭腑閰嶇疆浜嗙浉鍚岀殑娴撳害鍊?({concValue})锛乗n\n鍚屼竴鍏冪礌鐨勬爣鍑嗘憾娑叉祿搴﹀繀椤诲悇涓嶇浉鍚岋紝璇蜂慨鏀瑰悗閲嶈瘯銆?, "鏍囨恫娴撳害閲嶅鎷︽埅", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 妫€鏌ラ噸鍚?
            var duplicateNames = Samples.GroupBy(s => s.SampleName).Where(g => g.Count() > 1).Select(g => g.Key).ToList();
            if (duplicateNames.Count > 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"瀛樺湪閲嶅悕鐨勬牱鍝侊細{string.Join(", ", duplicateNames)}\n\n璇蜂慨鏀规牱鍝佸悕绉帮紝纭繚瀹冧滑鏄敮涓€鐨勶紒", "閿欒", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
                return;
            }

        // 銆愭柊闇€姹傘€戝簲鐢ㄦ椂锛屽皢鍏ㄥ眬鐨?Repeats 鍐欏叆姣忎釜 SampleItemModel
        foreach (var sample in Samples)
        {
            sample.Repeats = GlobalRepeats;
            sample.Interval = GlobalInterval;
        }

        // 灏嗗綋鍓嶅簭鍒楁繁鎷疯礉鍚庢帹閫佸埌娴佸姩娉ㄥ皠/娴嬮噺妯″潡锛岄槻姝笅娓告ā鍧楃殑淇敼姹℃煋鏈〉闈㈢殑鍘熷鏁版嵁
        var clonedSamples = Samples.Select(s => new SampleItemModel
        {
            SampleName = s.SampleName,
            Type = s.Type,
            Repeats = s.Repeats,
            Interval = s.Interval,
            Status = s.Status,
            ElementConcentrations = new ObservableCollection<ElementConcentrationModel>(
                s.ElementConcentrations.Select(c => new ElementConcentrationModel
                {
                    ElementName = c.ElementName,
                    ConcentrationValue = c.ConcentrationValue,
                    MeasuredIntensity = c.MeasuredIntensity,
                    MeasuredRsd = c.MeasuredRsd
                }))
        }).ToList();

        WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SampleSequenceChangedMessage(clonedSamples, _isDirtyOverwrite));
        _isDirtyOverwrite = false;
        
        // 鏍囪宸插簲鐢?
        IsSequenceApplied = true;

        MessageBox.Show("杩涙牱搴忓垪宸叉垚鍔熶笅鍙戣嚦娴嬮噺妯″潡锛乗n鐐瑰嚮纭畾鍚庡皢涓烘偍鑷姩璺宠浆鑷虫祴鏍峰垎鏋愮晫闈€?, "鎴愬姛", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);

        // 璺宠浆鍒版祴鏍峰垎鏋?
        WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new NavigateMessage("AnalysisWorkstation"));
    }

    // --- 绉佹湁杈呭姪 ---

    private SampleItemModel CreateNewSample(SampleType type, string name)
    {
            var sample = new SampleItemModel { Type = type, SampleName = name };
            foreach (var el in _activeElements)
            {
                sample.ElementConcentrations.Add(new ElementConcentrationModel { ElementName = el, ConcentrationValue = "" });
            }
            return sample;
        }

        private void RenameSampleSmartly(SampleItemModel item)
        {
            // 瀹夊叏鎷︽埅
            // 濡傛灉褰撳墠鏍峰搧瀹炰緥灏氭湭鍔犲叆鍒板綋鍓嶇殑娲诲姩 Samples 鍒楄〃涓紙渚嬪姝ｅ浜庡弽搴忓垪鍖栥€丆SV瑙ｆ瀽鎴栧厠闅嗚繃绋嬩腑锛夛紝
            // 蹇呴』鐩存帴杩斿洖锛屼繚鐣欏叾鍘熸湁鐨?SampleName 灞炴€э紝涓ョ鎵ц鑷姩閲嶅懡鍚嶅姭鎸併€?
            if (Samples == null || !Samples.Contains(item)) return;

            // 鏍规嵁绫诲瀷鑷姩缂栧彿閫昏緫
            string prefix = item.Type switch
            {
                SampleType.绌虹櫧 => "BLK-",
                SampleType.鏍囨恫 => "STD-",
                _ => "寰呮祴鏍?"
            };

            int maxIndex = 0;
            foreach (var s in Samples)
            {
                if (s != item && s.SampleName.StartsWith(prefix))
                {
                    string numPart = s.SampleName.Substring(prefix.Length);
                    if (int.TryParse(numPart, out int idx) && idx > maxIndex) maxIndex = idx;
                }
            }
            item.SampleName = $"{prefix}{maxIndex + 1}";
        }

        public void CheckDuplicateStandardConcentration()
        {
            var standardSamples = Samples.Where(s => s.Type == SampleType.鏍囨恫).ToList();
            if (standardSamples.Count <= 1) return;

            var elementNames = standardSamples.First().ElementConcentrations.Select(c => c.ElementName).ToList();
            foreach (var elementName in elementNames)
            {
                var concentrations = new HashSet<double>();
                foreach (var sample in standardSamples)
                {
                    var concModel = sample.ElementConcentrations.FirstOrDefault(c => c.ElementName == elementName);
                    if (concModel != null && double.TryParse(concModel.ConcentrationValue, out double concValue))
                    {
                        if (!concentrations.Add(concValue))
                        {
                            Application.Current.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(() =>
                            {
                                MessageBox.Show($"鍏冪礌 '{elementName}' 鍦ㄥ涓爣鍑嗘憾娑蹭腑閰嶇疆浜嗙浉鍚岀殑娴撳害鍊?({concValue})锛乗n\n鍚屼竴鍏冪礌鐨勬爣鍑嗘憾娑叉祿搴﹀繀椤诲悇涓嶇浉鍚岋紝璇蜂慨鏀广€?, "鏍囨恫娴撳害閲嶅", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                                concModel.ConcentrationValue = string.Empty;
                            }));
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

        /// JSON 閰嶇疆绠＄悊鏈嶅姟銆?        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>
        /// 瀹炴椂鍏夎氨鍗曞抚淇濆瓨鐨勯粯璁ゅ鍑烘枃浠跺す璺緞銆?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        private string _singleSaveExportPath = string.Empty;

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏃跺簭鍥炬暟鎹嚜鍔ㄤ繚瀛樼殑鐩綍璺緞銆?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        private string _timeSeriesSaveDirectory = string.Empty;

        #endregion

        #region 4. 鍒濆鍖?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鏋勯€犺缃〉闈㈣鍥炬ā鍨嬨€?        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="serialService">涓插彛鏈嶅姟銆?/param>
        /// <param name="configService">閰嶇疆鏈嶅姟銆?/param>
        public SettingsViewModel(ISerialPortService serialService, JsonConfigService configService)
        {
            _serialService = serialService;
            _configService = configService;

            // 娉ㄥ唽涓插彛搴曞眰鐘舵€佸彉鏇寸殑鍥炶皟
            _serialService.ConnectionStatusChanged += OnConnectionStatusChanged;

            // 鎭㈠鍘嗗彶閰嶇疆鐘舵€?            var config = _configService.Load();
            IsSpectrometerEnabled = config.IsSpectrometerEnabled;
            IsAutoReigniteEnabled = config.IsAutoReigniteEnabled;
            IgnitionDelaySeconds = config.IgnitionDelaySeconds;
            IgnitionSpeed = config.IgnitionSpeed;

            // 璺緞鍒濆鍖栫瓥鐣ワ細鑻ラ厤缃负绌猴紝榛樿鎸囧悜妗岄潰
            SingleSaveExportPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.SingleSaveExportPath)
                ? Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop)
                : config.SingleSaveExportPath;

            TimeSeriesSaveDirectory = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.TimeSeriesSaveDirectory)
                ? Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop)
                : config.TimeSeriesSaveDirectory;

            // 鍒濆鍒锋柊鐗╃悊涓插彛鍒楄〃
            RefreshPorts();
        }

        #endregion

        #region 5. 灞炴€у彉鏇存嫤鎴櫒 (涓氬姟閫昏緫鑱斿姩)

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎷︽埅鍏夎氨浠紑鍏崇姸鎬佸彉鏇淬€?        /// 鑱岃矗锛氬悓姝ユ寔涔呭寲閰嶇疆锛屽苟椹卞姩纭欢绠＄悊鍣ㄦ墽琛屽紓姝ヨ繛鏂€昏緫銆?        /// </summary>
        partial void OnIsSpectrometerEnabledChanged(bool value)
        {
            var config = _configService.Load();
            config.IsSpectrometerEnabled = value;
            _configService.Save(config);

            if (value)
            {
                // 寮€鍚細鍚姩寮傛纭欢鍙戠幇涓庡垵濮嬪寲娴佺▼
                _ = SpectrometerManager.Instance.DiscoverAndInitDevicesAsync();
            }
            else
            {
                // 鍏抽棴锛氬湪鍚庡彴绾跨▼鎵ц鏂紑娓呯悊锛岄槻姝㈢敱浜?USB 鍙ユ焺閲婃斁瀵艰嚧鐨?UI 鍝嶅簲杩熼挐
                _ = Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    SpectrometerManager.Instance.DisconnectAll();
                });
            }

            // 鏋勫缓骞挎挱娑堟伅锛岄€氱煡绯荤粺鍏朵綑缁勪欢鍏夎氨鎬诲紑鍏崇姸鎬佸彉鍖?            var globalStatusConfig = new SpectrometerConfig
            {
                SerialNumber = "GLOBAL_SWITCH",
                IsConnected = value
            };

            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Send(new SpectrometerStatusMessage(globalStatusConfig));
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎷︽埅鐐圭伀寮€鍏崇姸鎬佸彉鏇淬€?        /// </summary>
        partial void OnIsAutoReigniteEnabledChanged(bool value)
        {
            var config = _configService.Load();
            config.IsAutoReigniteEnabled = value;
            _configService.Save(config);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎷︽埅鐐圭伀寤舵椂鍙傛暟鍙樻洿銆?        /// 鑱岃矗锛氬己鍒舵墽琛屾暟鍊肩墿鐞嗚竟鐣岄檺鍒跺苟鎵ц鎸佷箙鍖栥€?        /// </summary>
        partial void OnIgnitionDelaySecondsChanged(double value)
        {
            // 鐗╃悊瀹夊叏杈圭晫锛?.1s - 5.0s
            if (value < 0.1) IgnitionDelaySeconds = 0.1;
            else if (value > 5.0) IgnitionDelaySeconds = 5.0;
            else
            {
                var config = _configService.Load();
                config.IgnitionDelaySeconds = Math.Round(value, 1); // 淇濊瘉涓€绉掍竴浣嶇簿搴?                _configService.Save(config);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎷︽埅鐐圭伀杞€熷弬鏁板彉鏇淬€?        /// 鑱岃矗锛氬己鍒舵墽琛屾暟鍊肩墿鐞嗚竟鐣岄檺鍒跺苟鎵ц鎸佷箙鍖栥€?        /// </summary>
        partial void OnIgnitionSpeedChanged(short value)
        {
            // 鐗╃悊瀹夊叏杈圭晫锛?0 - 100
            if (value < 50) IgnitionSpeed = 50;
            else if (value > 100) IgnitionSpeed = 100;
            else
            {
                var config = _configService.Load();
                config.IgnitionSpeed = value;
                _configService.Save(config);
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region 6. 涓插彛閫氳閫昏緫

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍝嶅簲搴曞眰杩炴帴鐘舵€佸彉鏇淬€?        /// </summary>
        private void OnConnectionStatusChanged(bool isOpen)
        {
            Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(UpdateStatusUI);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍒锋柊鍙敤涓插彛鍒楄〃銆?        /// 鍖呭惈鑷姩灏濊瘯杩炴帴閫昏緫銆?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void RefreshPorts()
        {
            var ports = _serialService.GetAvailablePorts();
            AvailablePorts.Clear();
            foreach (var port in ports)
            {
                AvailablePorts.Add(port);
            }

            // 鑻ュ綋鍓嶆湭杩炴帴涓旀娴嬪埌涓插彛锛屾墽琛岃嚜鍔ㄩ噸杩為€昏緫
            if (!_serialService.IsOpen)
            {
                _serialService.AutoConnect();
            }

            // 鏇存柊閫変腑椤规樉绀?            if (_serialService.IsOpen && _serialService.CurrentPortName != null && AvailablePorts.Contains(_serialService.CurrentPortName))
            {
                SelectedPort = _serialService.CurrentPortName;
            }
            else if (AvailablePorts.Count > 0)
            {
                SelectedPort = AvailablePorts[0];
            }
            else
            {
                SelectedPort = null;
            }

            UpdateStatusUI();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎵ц鎵嬪姩涓插彛杩炴帴璇锋眰銆?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void Connect()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedPort)) return;

            // 鐘舵€侀槻閲嶏細鑻ュ凡杩炴帴褰撳墠閫変腑鐨勪覆鍙ｅ垯蹇界暐
            if (_serialService.IsOpen && _serialService.CurrentPortName == SelectedPort) return;

            // 浜掓枼锛氬厛鍏抽棴鏃ц繛鎺ュ啀灏濊瘯鏂拌繛鎺?            if (_serialService.IsOpen) _serialService.Close();

            if (!_serialService.Open(SelectedPort, BAUD_RATE))
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"鏃犳硶鎵撳紑涓插彛 {SelectedPort}锛岃璁惧鍙兘姝ｅ湪琚叾浠栫▼搴忓崰鐢ㄣ€?, "杩炴帴鍐茬獊", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }

            UpdateStatusUI();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍚屾杩炴帴鐘舵€佹枃鏈嚦 UI銆?        /// </summary>
        private void UpdateStatusUI()
        {
            StatusText = _serialService.IsOpen
                ? $"宸茶繛鎺?({_serialService.CurrentPortName ?? "鏈煡"})"
                : "鏈繛鎺?;
        }

        #endregion

        #region 7. 瀵煎嚭璺緞绠＄悊

        /// <summary>
        /// 娴忚骞朵繚瀛樺崟娆″厜璋变繚瀛樿矾寰勩€?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void BrowseExportPath()
        {
            var dialog = new Ookii.Dialogs.Wpf.VistaFolderBrowserDialog
            {
                Description = "閫夋嫨鍗曟閲囨牱鏁版嵁鐨勫鍑虹洰褰?,
                UseDescriptionForTitle = true,
                SelectedPath = SingleSaveExportPath
            };

            if (dialog.ShowDialog() == true)
            {
                SingleSaveExportPath = dialog.SelectedPath;
                var config = _configService.Load();
                config.SingleSaveExportPath = dialog.SelectedPath;
                _configService.Save(config);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 娴忚骞朵繚瀛樻椂搴忓浘鑷姩澶囦唤璺緞銆?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void BrowseTimeSeriesDirectory()
        {
            using var dialog = new System.Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog
            {
                Description = "閫夋嫨鏃跺簭鐩戞祴鏁版嵁鐨勫浠界洰褰?,
                UseDescriptionForTitle = true,
                SelectedPath = TimeSeriesSaveDirectory
            };

            if (dialog.ShowDialog() == System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK)
            {
                TimeSeriesSaveDirectory = dialog.SelectedPath;
                var config = _configService.Load();
                config.TimeSeriesSaveDirectory = TimeSeriesSaveDirectory;
                _configService.Save(config);
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region 8. 鍙傛暟绮剧粏鍖栬皟鏁存寚浠?
        /// <summary>
        /// 澧炲姞鐐圭伀寤舵椂鏃堕棿锛?0.1s锛夈€?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void IncreaseIgnitionDelay()
        {
            IgnitionDelaySeconds = Math.Round(Math.Min(5.0, IgnitionDelaySeconds + 0.1), 1);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍑忓皯鐐圭伀寤舵椂鏃堕棿锛?0.1s锛夈€?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void DecreaseIgnitionDelay()
        {
            IgnitionDelaySeconds = Math.Round(Math.Max(0.1, IgnitionDelaySeconds - 0.1), 1);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 澧炲姞鐐圭伀杞€燂紙+1锛夈€?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void IncreaseIgnitionSpeed()
        {
            IgnitionSpeed = (short)Math.Min(100, IgnitionSpeed + 1);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍑忓皯鐐圭伀杞€燂紙-1锛夈€?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void DecreaseIgnitionSpeed()
        {
            IgnitionSpeed = (short)Math.Max(50, IgnitionSpeed - 1);
        }

        #endregion
    }
}

        /// <summary>

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏋勯€犲厜璋变华瑙嗗浘妯″瀷銆?        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="model">鍏夎氨浠厤缃暟鎹ā鍨嬨€?/param>
        /// <param name="service">鍏夎氨浠‖浠堕┍鍔ㄦ湇鍔°€?/param>
        public SpectrometerViewModel(SpectrometerConfig model, ISpectrometerService service)
        {
            _model = model ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(model));
            _service = service;

            // 鏋勯€犳椂鍚屾鐗╃悊 Model 鏁版嵁
            UpdateFromModel();

            // 娉ㄥ唽寮卞紩鐢ㄦ秷鎭闃咃細鐩戝惉搴曞眰纭欢浜х敓鐨勫厜璋辨暟鎹寘
            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SpectralDataMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                // 鏍稿績璺敱閫昏緫锛氬鏋滃綋鍓嶆湭缁戝畾鐗瑰畾搴忓垪鍙凤紝鎴栬€呭簭鍒楀彿鍖归厤锛屾垨鑰呭浜庤仈鏈烘ā寮忥紝鍧囨帴鍙楁暟鎹粯鍒?                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(this.SerialNumber) || m.Value.SourceDeviceSerial == this.SerialNumber || this.SerialNumber == "鑱旀満妯″紡")
                {
                    RequestPlotUpdate?.Invoke(m.Value);
                }
            });

            // 鐩戝惉鑷姩鍖栧簭鍒楃瓑澶栭儴缁勪欢淇敼纭欢鍙傛暟鐨勫悓姝ユ秷鎭?            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<HardwareConfigChangedMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                // 濡傛灉褰撳墠鏈粦瀹氱壒瀹氬簭鍒楀彿锛屾垨鑰呭簭鍒楀彿鍖归厤锛屾垨鑰呭浜庤仈鏈烘ā寮忥紝鍧囨帴鍙楁洿鏂?                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(this.SerialNumber) || m.SerialNumber == this.SerialNumber || this.SerialNumber == "鑱旀満妯″紡")
                {
                    System.Windows.Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                    {
                        IntegrationTimeMs = m.IntegrationTimeMs;
                        AveragingCount = m.AveragingCount;
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 浠庣墿鐞?Model 鍏ㄩ噺鏇存柊鍩虹鏁版嵁鑷?ViewModel 灞炴€с€?        /// </summary>
        public void UpdateFromModel()
        {
            SerialNumber = _model.SerialNumber;
            IsConnected = _model.IsConnected;
            IntegrationTimeMs = _model.IntegrationTimeMs;
            AveragingCount = _model.AveragingCount;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 娉ㄥ叆纭欢鎺у埗鏈嶅姟銆?        /// </summary>
        public void SetService(ISpectrometerService service)
        {
            _service = service;
        }

        /// <summary> 鎷︽埅绉垎鏃堕棿鍙樻洿骞跺悓姝ヨ嚦 Model銆?</summary>
        partial void OnIntegrationTimeMsChanged(float value) => _model.IntegrationTimeMs = value;

        /// <summary> 鎷︽埅骞冲潎娆℃暟鍙樻洿骞跺悓姝ヨ嚦 Model銆?</summary>
        partial void OnAveragingCountChanged(uint value) => _model.AveragingCount = value;

        #endregion

        #region 6. 鍙傛暟淇敼閫昏緫 (寮傛閲嶅惎鏈哄埗)

        /// <summary>
        /// 寮傛淇敼绉垎鏃堕棿鍛戒护銆?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ChangeIntegrationTimeAsync(string timeStr)
        {
            if (float.TryParse(timeStr, out float newTime))
            {
                IntegrationTimeMs = newTime;
                _model.IntegrationTimeMs = newTime;
                SaveConfigAction?.Invoke();

                await ApplyHardwareConfigurationAsync(newTime, AveragingCount);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 寮傛淇敼閲囨牱骞冲潎娆℃暟鍛戒护銆?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ChangeAveragingCountAsync(string countStr)
        {
            if (uint.TryParse(countStr, out uint newCount))
            {
                AveragingCount = newCount;
                _model.AveragingCount = newCount;
                SaveConfigAction?.Invoke();

                await ApplyHardwareConfigurationAsync(IntegrationTimeMs, newCount);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏍稿績閰嶇疆搴旂敤閫昏緫锛氭墽琛屸€滃仠姝?鏇存柊鍙傛暟-閲嶅惎鈥濇祦姘寸嚎銆?        /// </summary>
        private async Task ApplyHardwareConfigurationAsync(float integrationTime, uint averagingCount)
        {
            var devices = SpectrometerManager.Instance.Devices;
            if (devices.Count == 0) return;

            // 鍦ㄥ悗鍙版墽琛岀‖浠剁姸鎬佹満鎿嶄綔锛岄伩鍏嶉樆濉?UI 绾跨▼
            await Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                bool wasMeasuring = IsCurrentlyMeasuring;

                // 鐗╃悊鐔旀柇锛氫慨鏀瑰瘎瀛樺櫒鍓嶅繀椤诲仠姝㈡帹娴?                if (wasMeasuring) SpectrometerManager.Instance.StopAll();

                // 鍙傛暟閲嶅啓锛氬苟鍙戞洿鏂版墍鏈夌墿鐞嗚澶囩殑閰嶇疆
                var updateTasks = devices.Select(device => device.UpdateConfigurationAsync(integrationTime, averagingCount));
                await Task.WhenAll(updateTasks);

                // 鎭㈠娴嬮噺锛氬弬鏁板簲鐢ㄦ垚鍔熷悗閲嶅惎閲囬泦
                if (wasMeasuring) SpectrometerManager.Instance.StartAll();
            });
        }

        #endregion
    }
}

namespace GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels
{
    public partial class SyringePumpViewModel : DeviceBaseViewModel
    {
        #region 1. 渚濊禆鏈嶅姟

        private readonly GeneralDeviceService _generalService;
        private readonly JsonConfigService _configService;

        #endregion

        #region 2. 缁戝畾灞炴€?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鐣岄潰灞曠ず鐨勫綋鍓嶈瀹氳窛绂诲揩鐓с€?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private string _settingDistance = "0";

        /// <summary>
        /// 鐣岄潰灞曠ず鐨勫垵濮嬫柟鍚戞弿杩帮紙鍙嶅悜/姝ｅ悜锛夈€?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty] private string _directionText = "鍙嶅悜";

        #endregion

        public SyringePumpViewModel(GeneralDeviceService generalService, JsonConfigService configService)
        {
            _generalService = generalService;
            _configService = configService;
            DisplayName = "娉ㄥ皠娉?;
            UpdateUIFromConfig();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍚屾鏈湴閰嶇疆鑷?UI 鏂囨湰蹇収銆?        /// </summary>
        private void UpdateUIFromConfig()
        {
            var config = _configService.Load();
            SettingDistance = $"{config.LastSyringeDistance}";
            DirectionText = config.IsSyringeOutput ? "姝ｅ悜" : "鍙嶅悜";
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 鐩戝惉鍗＄墖鍚姩淇″彿锛岃Е鍙戣繘鏍疯嚜鍔ㄥ寲搴忓垪銆?        /// </summary>
        protected override async void OnPropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnPropertyChanged(e);

            if (e.PropertyName == nameof(IsRunning) && IsRunning)
            {
                // 1. 閿佸畾鎺у埗鏉冮檺
                IsBusy = true;

                var config = _configService.Load();
                short targetDistance = (short)config.LastSyringeDistance;
                bool initialPort = config.IsSyringeOutput;

                // 2. 鍔ㄦ€佽绠楀姩浣滄墍闇€鏃堕棿
                // 鍏紡锛?閲忕▼姣? * 4.5绉掑叏琛岀▼鏃堕棿 + 1绉掔墿鐞嗘儻鎬х紦鍐?                int delayMs = (int)((targetDistance / 3000.0) * 4500) + 1000;

                try
                {
                    // 姝ラ A锛氬惎鍔ㄦ娊娑插姩浣?                    ButtonText = "鎶?;
                    _generalService.ControlSyringePump(initialPort, targetDistance);
                    await Task.Delay(delayMs);

                    // 姝ラ B锛氬惎鍔ㄦ帹娑插姩浣滐紙杩斿洖鍘熺偣锛岀墿鐞嗛榾闂ㄨ嚜鍔ㄦ崲鍚戯級
                    ButtonText = "鎺?;
                    _generalService.ControlSyringePump(!initialPort, 0);
                    await Task.Delay(delayMs);
                }
                finally
                {
                    // 姝ラ C锛氭仮澶嶅垵濮嬬姸鎬?                    ButtonText = "鍚姩";
                    IsRunning = false;
                    IsBusy = false;
                }
            }
        }

        protected override void ExecuteOpenDetail()
        {
            if (IsBusy) return;
            var window = new SyringePumpSettingWindow();
            window.DataContext = new SyringePumpSettingViewModel(_configService, _configService.Load(), () => { window.Close(); UpdateUIFromConfig(); });
            window.Owner = Application.Current.MainWindow;
            window.ShowDialog();
        }
    }
}

 * 鏂囦欢鍚? TimeSeriesViewModel.cs
 * 鎻忚堪: 澶氶€氶亾鏃跺簭鐩戞祴瑙嗗浘妯″瀷銆傝礋璐ｅ厜璋辩壒寰佸嘲寮哄害闅忔椂闂村彉鍖栨洸绾跨殑鏁版嵁绠＄悊銆乁I 鑺傛祦娓叉煋璋冨害鍙婅嚜鍔ㄥ寲璁板綍瀵煎嚭銆? * 缁存姢鎸囧崡: 
 * 1. 閲囩敤鈥滃弻杞ㄦ暟鎹祦鈥濇灦鏋勶細UI 杞ㄩ亾閫氳繃 FIFO 鏈哄埗缁存寔杞婚噺鍖栫偣鏁帮紙10000鐐癸級锛屽悗鍙拌建閬撳垯鏃犻檺鍒剁紦鍐茬洿鑷?Excel 钀界洏銆? * 2. 搴曞眰纭欢鏁版嵁鍦ㄩ潪鎵樼绾跨▼涓珮棰戞敞鍏ワ紝閫氳繃閿佸畾姣忎釜閫氶亾鐨?SyncRoot 纭繚 View 灞傝鍙栨覆鏌撴椂涓嶅彂鐢熼泦鍚堝啿绐併€? */

namespace GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 鏃跺簭鍥剧洃娴嬭鍥炬ā鍨嬶細璐熻矗澶氶€氶亾鏁版嵁鐨勬车鍏ャ€佺紦瀛樹笌涓氬姟娴佽浆銆?    /// </summary>
    public partial class TimeSeriesViewModel : ObservableObject
    {
        #region 1. 渚濊禆鏈嶅姟涓庣姸鎬佸睘鎬?
        /// <summary>
        /// JSON 閰嶇疆绠＄悊鏈嶅姟銆?        /// 鐢ㄤ簬璇诲彇鏃跺簭鍥剧洃鎺ц妭鐐圭殑娉㈤暱閰嶇疆銆?        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>

        private readonly PeakTrackingService _peakTrackingService;

        /// <summary>
        /// 鏍稿績鐘舵€侊細褰撳墠鏄惁姝ｅ浜庡悗鍙拌繛缁噰闆嗗苟缂撳瓨鏁版嵁鐨勫綍鍒剁姸鎬併€?        /// 鍙樻洿鏃朵細绾ц仈瑙﹀彂 UI 鎸夐挳鏂囨湰銆佸浘鏍囧強棰滆壊鐨勭姸鎬佹洿鏂般€?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(RecordText))]
        [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(RecordIcon))]
        [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(RecordButtonColor))]
        private bool _isRecording;

        /// <summary>
        /// 瑙嗗浘甯冨眬妯″紡鏍囪瘑銆?        /// True 涓?2xN 鐨勭煩闃靛睍绀烘ā寮忥紝False 涓哄崟鍒楃旱鍚戞帓鍒楁ā寮忋€?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(ViewModeText))]
        [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(ViewModeIcon))]
        private bool _isMatrixView;

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍓嶇娓叉煋瀹瑰櫒锛圲niformGrid锛夌殑鍔ㄦ€佸垪鏁般€?        /// 鍐呴儴鏍规嵁瑙嗗浘妯″紡涓庨€氶亾鎬绘暟鑷姩璁＄畻銆?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        private int _gridColumns = 1;

        /// <summary>
        /// 閫氶亾鍥捐〃鏁版嵁椤归泦鍚堛€?        /// 姣忎竴椤瑰搴?UI 涓婂睍绀虹殑涓€涓瓙鍥捐〃锛堜竴涓壒瀹氱殑娉㈤暱杩借釜鐐癸級銆?        /// </summary>
        [ObservableProperty]
        private ObservableCollection<TimeSeriesChartItem> _charts = new();

        #endregion

        #region 2. 鍐呴儴璁℃椂涓庨珮鎬ц兘闄愬埗

        /// <summary>
        /// 鐩稿鏃堕棿楂樼簿搴﹁鏃跺櫒銆?        /// 鐢ㄤ簬涓哄綍鍒舵湡闂寸殑姣忎竴甯ф墦涓婁互绉掍负鍗曚綅鐨勬椂闂存埑锛圶 杞寸墿鐞嗗潗鏍囷級銆?        /// </summary>
        private readonly Stopwatch _stopwatch = new();

        /// <summary>
        /// 鐗╃悊闄愬埗锛氬墠绔?UI 娓叉煋鍏佽瀛樺湪鐨勬渶澶у巻鍙茬偣鏁般€?        /// 璁剧疆涓?10000 浠ュ钩琛￠暱鏃堕棿鐩戞祴鐨勮瑙夎繛缁€т笌绯荤粺鍐呭瓨璐熻浇銆?        /// </summary>
        private const int MAX_UI_POINTS = 10000;

        // --- UI 鎸夐挳鍔ㄦ€佹淳鐢熷睘鎬?---
        public string RecordText => IsRecording ? "鍋滄璁板綍" : "寮€濮嬭褰?;
        public string RecordIcon => IsRecording ? "StopCircle" : "PlayCircle";
        public string RecordButtonColor => IsRecording ? "#f44336" : "#673ab7";
        public string ViewModeText => IsMatrixView ? "鍒楄〃瑙嗗浘" : "鐭╅樀瑙嗗浘";
        public string ViewModeIcon => IsMatrixView ? "ViewSequential" : "ViewGrid";

        #endregion

        #region 3. 澶氱嚎绋嬪紓姝ョ紦瀛樺熀寤?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鏍稿績骞跺彂鍚屾閿併€?        /// 鐢ㄤ簬鍦ㄥ悗鍙板啓鍏ョ紦瀛樻睜涓庡墠绔竻鐞嗗鍑轰箣闂寸殑璧勬簮绔炰簤淇濇姢銆?        /// </summary>
        private readonly object _cacheLock = new object();

        /// <summary>
        /// 鍚庡彴鏃犻檺鏁版嵁缂撳瓨姹犮€?        /// 涓存椂瀛樺偍褰曞埗鏈熼棿鐨勬墍鏈夊厜璋卞揩鐓э紝寰呭仠姝㈠悗涓€娆℃€ф壒閲忓啓鍏?Excel銆?        /// </summary>
        private readonly List<Models.Spectrometer.TimeSeriesSnapshot> _timeSeriesCache = new();

        /// <summary>
        /// 褰曞埗鏈熻〃澶撮攣瀹氬揩鐓с€?        /// 璁板綍鍚姩鐬棿鐨勯€氶亾鍚嶇О鍒楄〃锛岄槻姝㈠鍑鸿繃绋嬩腑鐢变簬閰嶇疆鏇存敼瀵艰嚧鐨?Excel 鏍煎紡閿欎贡銆?        /// </summary>
        private readonly List<string> _lockedTrackPointNames = new();

        /// <summary>
        /// 纭欢鍙傛暟涓婁笅鏂囷細绉垎鏃堕棿锛坢s锛夈€?        /// </summary>
        private float _currentIntegrationTime;

        /// <summary>
        /// 纭欢鍙傛暟涓婁笅鏂囷細骞冲潎娆℃暟銆?        /// </summary>
        private uint _currentAveragingCount;

        #endregion

        #region 4. 鏋勯€犲垵濮嬪寲涓庢秷鎭闃?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鏋勯€犳椂搴忓浘瑙嗗浘妯″瀷骞舵敞鍐岄珮棰戞暟鎹€荤嚎鐩戝惉銆?        /// </summary>
        public TimeSeriesViewModel(JsonConfigService configService, PeakTrackingService peakTrackingService)
        {
            _configService = configService;
            _peakTrackingService = peakTrackingService;

            // 娉ㄥ唽瀵瑰簳灞傚厜璋辨秷鎭殑蹇€熸嫤鎴?            WeakReferenceMessenger.Default.Register<SpectralDataMessage>(this, (r, m) =>
            {
                // 鏇存柊褰撳墠纭欢鐜鍙傛暟锛岀敤浜?Excel 瀵煎嚭淇℃伅澶?                _currentIntegrationTime = m.IntegrationTime;
                _currentAveragingCount = m.AveragingCount;

                // 鑻ュ浜庡綍鍒朵腑锛屽垯鍚姩鍙岃建娉垫按寮曟搸
                if (IsRecording) InjectDataToCharts();
            });

            // 鍒濆浠庨厤缃枃浠跺姞杞藉凡淇濆瓨鐨勭洃鎺ц妭鐐?            ReloadChartsFromConfig();
        }

        #endregion

        #region 5. 鏁版嵁娉靛紩鎿?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鏍稿績鏁版嵁娉碉細灏嗙壒寰佸嘲鏈嶅姟涓殑鏈€鏂扮墿鐞嗗潗鏍囨媶瑙ｃ€佹槧灏勫苟鍒嗗彂鑷冲悇鍥捐〃閫氶亾銆?        /// 鑱岃矗锛氬悓姝ユ墽琛屽墠绔?FIFO 鏇存柊涓庡悗鍙版棤闄愮紦瀛樿拷鍔犮€?        /// </summary>
        private void InjectDataToCharts()
        {
            double currentTime = _stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds;
            double[] currentIntensities = new double[Charts.Count];
            int activePeakCount = 0;

            // 姝ラ A锛氶亶鍘嗛€氶亾闆嗗悎锛屾彁鍙栧搴旀尝闀跨殑瀹炴椂寮哄害
            for (int i = 0; i < Charts.Count; i++)
            {
                var chart = Charts[i];
                var matchedPeak = _peakTrackingService.TrackedPeaks
                    .FirstOrDefault(p => Math.Abs(p.BaseWavelength - chart.TargetWavelength) < 0.01);

                if (matchedPeak != null)
                {
                    currentIntensities[i] = matchedPeak.CurrentIntensity;
                    activePeakCount++;

                    // 姝ラ B锛歎I 杞ㄩ亾鏇存柊 (甯︾嚎绋嬮殧绂讳繚鎶?
                    lock (chart.SyncRoot)
                    {
                        chart.TimePoints.Add(currentTime);
                        chart.IntensityPoints.Add(matchedPeak.CurrentIntensity);

                        // FIFO 娣樻卑閫昏緫锛氳嫢鐐规暟婧㈠嚭锛屼粠闃熼绉婚櫎鏈€鑰佺殑鏁版嵁
                        if (chart.TimePoints.Count > MAX_UI_POINTS)
                        {
                            chart.TimePoints.RemoveAt(0);
                            chart.IntensityPoints.RemoveAt(0);
                        }
                        chart.HasNewData = true; // 瑙﹀彂 UI 娓叉煋鑴忔鏌?                    }
                }
            }

            // 姝ラ C锛氬紓甯稿畨鍏ㄦ嫤鎴?            // 鑻ョ洃娴嬪埌涓荤晫闈㈡墍鏈夎拷韪偣鍧囪鍒犻櫎锛岀珛鍗冲己琛屼繚瀛樺凡鏈夋暟鎹苟鍋滄锛岄槻姝骇鐢熼浂鏁版嵁鍨冨溇甯с€?            if (activePeakCount == 0)
            {
                Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => { if (IsRecording) _ = ToggleRecordingAsync(); });
                return;
            }

            // 姝ラ D锛氬悗鍙拌建閬撴洿鏂?(鍘嬪叆 Excel 瀵煎嚭闃熷垪)
            lock (_cacheLock)
            {
                _timeSeriesCache.Add(new Models.Spectrometer.TimeSeriesSnapshot
                {
                    Timestamp = DateTime.Now,
                    Intensities = currentIntensities
                });
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region 6. 褰曞埗鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡绠＄悊

        /// <summary>
        /// 鎵ц鏃跺簭璁板綍鐨勭姸鎬佸垏鎹€?        /// 鑱岃矗锛氭帶鍒朵换鍔″惎鍔ㄣ€佸唴瀛樺垏鍓层€佸紓姝ヨ惤鐩樹互鍙?Excel 鏈嶅姟璋冨害銆?        /// </summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ToggleRecordingAsync()
        {
            if (IsRecording)
            {
                // --- 娴佺▼ A锛氬仠姝㈠綍鍒跺苟瀹夊叏钀界洏 ---
                IsRecording = false;
                _stopwatch.Stop();

                List<Models.Spectrometer.TimeSeriesSnapshot> dataToSave;
                List<string> columnsToSave;

                // 鍘熷瓙绾у壀鍒囨搷浣滐細鐬棿娓呯┖缂撳瓨骞惰浆绉绘寚閽堬紝闅忓悗绔嬪嵆閲婃斁閿佷互鎭㈠绯荤粺鍝嶅簲
                lock (_cacheLock)
                {
                    dataToSave = _timeSeriesCache.ToList();
                    columnsToSave = _lockedTrackPointNames.ToList();
                    _timeSeriesCache.Clear();
                    _lockedTrackPointNames.Clear();
                }

                if (dataToSave.Count > 0)
                {
                    var config = _configService.Load();
                    string dirPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.TimeSeriesSaveDirectory)
                        ? Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop)
                        : config.TimeSeriesSaveDirectory;

                    string fullPath = Path.Combine(dirPath, $"TimeSeries_Auto_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}.xlsx");

                    using var exportService = new ExcelExportService();
                    bool success = await exportService.ExportTimeSeriesBulkAsync(
                        fullPath, columnsToSave, dataToSave, _currentIntegrationTime, _currentAveragingCount);

                    Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                    {
                        if (success) MessageBox.Show($"璁板綍宸插畬鎴愬苟瀵煎嚭鑷筹細\n{fullPath}", "鑷姩瀵煎嚭鎴愬姛", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                        else MessageBox.Show("瀵煎嚭澶辫触锛氱洰鏍囨枃浠跺彲鑳藉凡琚墜鍔ㄦ墦寮€鍗犵敤銆?, "IO 閿欒", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    });
                }
            }
            else
            {
                // --- 娴佺▼ B锛氬垵濮嬪寲褰曞埗涓婁笅鏂?---
                // 鍚姩鏍￠獙1锛氭槸鍚﹀湪涓荤晫闈㈠宄?                if (_peakTrackingService.TrackedPeaks.Count == 0)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("涓荤晫闈㈠皻鏈爣璁颁换浣曠壒寰佸嘲锛岃鍏堣繑鍥炰富鐣岄潰杩涜瀵诲嘲鏍囪銆?, "鎿嶄綔鎷掔粷", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                    return;
                }

                // 鍚姩鏍￠獙2锛氬鍒扮殑宄版槸鍚﹀湪閲囨牱閰嶇疆涓叧鑱?                int activeCount = Charts.Count(c => _peakTrackingService.TrackedPeaks.Any(p => Math.Abs(p.BaseWavelength - c.TargetWavelength) < 0.01));
                if (activeCount == 0)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("涓荤晫闈㈠凡鏍囪鐗瑰緛宄帮紝浣嗗皻鏈湪銆愰噰鏍烽厤缃€戜腑杩涜鍏宠仈銆傝鐐瑰嚮鍙充笂瑙掋€愰噰鏍烽厤缃€戣繘琛屽叧鑱旇缃€?, "鎿嶄綔鎷掔粷", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                    return;
                }

                // 閿佸畾褰撳墠涓氬姟蹇収
                lock (_cacheLock)
                {
                    _timeSeriesCache.Clear();
                    _lockedTrackPointNames.Clear();
                    _lockedTrackPointNames.AddRange(Charts.Select(c => c.Title));
                }

                // 娓呯┖ UI 鍘嗗彶娉㈠舰锛屽苟寮哄埗鎺ㄥ叆 (0, 0) 浣滀负缁樺浘璧风偣
                foreach (var chart in Charts)
                {
                    lock (chart.SyncRoot) 
                    { 
                        chart.TimePoints.Clear(); 
                        chart.IntensityPoints.Clear(); 

                        // 婊¤冻棣栦釜鐐逛粠 0 缁樺埗鐨勯渶姹?                        chart.TimePoints.Add(0);
                        chart.IntensityPoints.Add(0);

                        chart.HasNewData = true; 
                    }
                }

                _stopwatch.Restart();
                IsRecording = true;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 绱ф€ラ潤榛樿惤鐩橀€昏緫銆?        /// 涓撲緵 App 鎰忓閫€鍑烘椂璋冪敤锛岄€氳繃寮哄埗鍚屾闃诲纭繚鍐呭瓨鏁版嵁鍦ㄨ繘绋嬬粨鏉熷墠鍐欏叆妗岄潰銆?        /// </summary>
        public void EmergencySave()
        {
            if (!IsRecording || _timeSeriesCache.Count == 0) return;
            _stopwatch.Stop();

            List<Models.Spectrometer.TimeSeriesSnapshot> dataToSave;
            List<string> columnsToSave;

            lock (_cacheLock) { dataToSave = _timeSeriesCache.ToList(); columnsToSave = _lockedTrackPointNames.ToList(); }

            var config = _configService.Load();
            string dirPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.TimeSeriesSaveDirectory) ? Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop) : config.TimeSeriesSaveDirectory;
            string fullPath = Path.Combine(dirPath, $"TimeSeries_Auto_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}_Exit.xlsx");

            using var exportService = new ExcelExportService();
            // 浣跨敤鍚屾闃诲锛岀‘淇濆湪 Windows 寮哄埗鏉€杩涚▼鍓嶅畬鎴愮鐩樻搷浣?            exportService.ExportTimeSeriesBulkAsync(fullPath, columnsToSave, dataToSave, _currentIntegrationTime, _currentAveragingCount).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
        }

        #endregion

        #region 7. UI 浜や簰鎺у埗

        /// <summary> 鎷︽埅鐭╅樀瑙嗗浘鏍囪瘑鍙樻洿锛屽姩鎬侀噸绠楁帓鐗堝垪鏁般€?</summary>
        partial void OnIsMatrixViewChanged(bool value) => UpdateGridColumns();

        /// <summary> 鍒囨崲瑙嗗浘妯″紡鎸囦护銆?</summary>
        [RelayCommand] private void ToggleViewMode() => IsMatrixView = !IsMatrixView;

        /// <summary> 閲嶇畻鍒楁暟锛氱煩闃垫ā寮忎笅涓旈€氶亾鏁板ぇ浜?1 鏃讹紝閲囩敤鍙屽垪鎺掔増銆?</summary>
        private void UpdateGridColumns() => GridColumns = (IsMatrixView && Charts.Count > 1) ? 2 : 1;

        /// <summary> 寮瑰嚭鏃跺簭鐩戞帶鑺傜偣锛堟尝闀?棰滆壊锛夌殑閰嶇疆瀵硅瘽妗嗐€?</summary>
        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenSamplingConfig()
        {
            var window = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.Views.Dialogs.TimeSeriesSamplingConfigWindow();
            window.DataContext = new GD_ControlCenter_WPF.ViewModels.Dialogs.TimeSeriesSamplingConfigViewModel(_configService, _peakTrackingService, () => window.DialogResult = true);
            window.Owner = Application.Current.MainWindow;
            
            if (window.ShowDialog() == true)
            {
                ReloadChartsFromConfig(); // 瀵硅瘽妗嗗叧闂笖搴旂敤鍚庢墠鍒锋柊鐗╃悊閫氶亾鍒楄〃
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 閫氶亾閲嶈浇閫昏緫锛氭牴鎹湰鍦?JSON 閰嶇疆鍔ㄦ€佹瀯寤烘椂搴忕洃鎺у疄浣撱€?        /// 鍏峰鍙屽悜杩囨护锛氬繀椤诲湪閰嶇疆涓嬀閫夛紝涓斿綋鍓嶄富鐣岄潰蹇呴』宸插紑鍚鐗瑰緛宄扮殑瀵诲嘲杩借釜銆?        /// </summary>
        private void ReloadChartsFromConfig()
        {
            var config = _configService.Load();
            Charts.Clear();

            var validNodes = config.TimeSeriesSampleNodes?
                .Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n.Name) && n.SelectedPeakX.HasValue)
                .Where(n => _peakTrackingService.TrackedPeaks.Any(p => Math.Abs(p.BaseWavelength - n.SelectedPeakX!.Value) < 0.01))
                .ToList();

            if (validNodes == null || validNodes.Count == 0)
            {
                Charts.Add(new TimeSeriesChartItem("绛夊緟鐗瑰緛宄版爣璁?..", 0, 0));
            }
            else
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < validNodes.Count; i++)
                {
                    var node = validNodes[i];
                    Charts.Add(new TimeSeriesChartItem($"{node.Name} ({node.SelectedPeakX!.Value:F2} nm)", node.SelectedPeakX!.Value, i));
                }
            }
            UpdateGridColumns();
        }

        #endregion

        #region 8. 宓屽绫伙細瀛愰€氶亾鍥捐〃鏁版嵁婧?
        /// <summary>
        /// 鍗曚釜鐩戞祴閫氶亾鐨勬暟鎹壙杞戒綋銆?        /// 灏佽浜嗘姌绾垮浘鐨勭偣闆嗐€佺粏绮掑害鍚屾閿佷互鍙?ScottPlot 绾挎潯閰嶈壊鏂规銆?        /// </summary>
        public partial class TimeSeriesChartItem : ObservableObject
        {
            /// <summary> 閫氶亾鏄剧ず鍚嶇О锛堝惈娉㈤暱璇存槑锛夈€?</summary>
            [ObservableProperty] private string _title;

            /// <summary> 鍏宠仈鐨勭墿鐞嗙洰鏍囨尝闀匡紙nm锛夈€?</summary>
            [ObservableProperty] private double _targetWavelength;

            /// <summary> 鏃堕棿鍧愭爣鐐归泦 (X)銆?</summary>
            public List<double> TimePoints { get; } = new();

            /// <summary> 鐗╃悊寮哄害鐐归泦 (Y)銆?</summary>
            public List<double> IntensityPoints { get; } = new();

            /// <summary> 閫氶亾绾т笓鐢ㄩ攣锛氶槻姝㈠悗鍙?Inject 鍐欏叆涓?UI 娓叉煋璇诲彇鍙戠敓纰版挒銆?</summary>
            public object SyncRoot { get; } = new object();

            /// <summary> 绯荤粺鑷姩鍒嗛厤鐨勯珮瀵规瘮搴︾粯鍥鹃鑹层€?</summary>
            public ScottPlot.Color LineColor { get; }

            /// <summary> 鑴忔暟鎹爣璇嗭細鐢?View 灞傚畾鏃跺櫒杞锛屽垽瀹氬綋鍓嶉€氶亾鏄惁闇€瑕侀噸缁樸€?</summary>
            public bool HasNewData { get; set; } = false;

            public TimeSeriesChartItem(string title, double targetWavelength, int colorIndex)
            {
                Title = title;
                TargetWavelength = targetWavelength;

                // 绉戝缁樺浘鏍囧噯鑹叉澘锛堥珮瀵规瘮搴︼級
                string[] hexColors = { "#1976D2", "#D32F2F", "#388E3C", "#F57C00", "#7B1FA2", "#0097A7", "#E64A19", "#689F38", "#C2185B", "#5D4037" };
                LineColor = ScottPlot.Color.FromHex(hexColors[colorIndex % hexColors.Length]);
            }
        }

        #endregion
    }
